It\n// returns true when every packet's change-set is an accepted head and Verified\n// \u2014 the `built: work-graph` conjunct. The frontier advances across drive\n// iterations: a packet becomes ready when its ancestors' change-sets are\n// admitted, and a quarantined packet's siblings proceed around it.\nfunc (s *Session) planWorkGraphBuild(state *State, request *RequestState, wgKind string, planned map[string]bool, steps *[]planStep, ensure func(kind, conjunctKind string) bool) bool {\n\t// A legacy subject-grain Change Set may carry the authored packet anchor\n\t// needed to recover a v1 Work Graph. Do\n\t\t\t// not let the older accepted head remove the packet from the\n\t\t\t// frontier: the frontier is where the candidate-based revision pins\n\t\t\t// its Review Ledger (or mechanical refusal) as diagnostics.\n\t\t\tif identityState, ok := state.Identities[csID]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tif candidate := latestCandidate(identityState); candidate != nil && candidate.Seq > head.Seq {\n\t\t\t\t\tif legacyEraCandidate(state, candidate) || !activeRequestMatchesCandidateContext(state, request, candidate) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t_, reviewRefused := s.reviewRefused(state, identityState, candidate)\n\t\t\t\t\t_, checksRefused := packetCheckRefusal(state, csID, candidate)\n\t\t\t\t\tif reviewRefused || checksRefused {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// An accepted head the teeth conclusively reject is NOT built: it\n\t\t\t// stranded its work-graph (dual-signal never satisfies) and must\n\t\t\t// re-enter the frontier as a revision (below), not be counted done.\n\t\t\tif _, rejected := s.verificationRejected(state, csID, head); !rejected {\n\t\t\t\tbuilt[p.ID] = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Dispatch builds for dependency-ready packets not yet built and not in\n\t// flight. {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tplanned[stepID] = true\n\t\t*steps = append(*steps, planStep{\n\t\t\tStepID:        stepID,\n\t\t\tPassID:        \"build\",\n\t\t\tSubject:       csID,\n\t\t\tInputs:        []string{wgIdentity},\n\t\t\tPacket:        pid,\n\t\t\tReason:        reason,\n\t\t\tCheckDetail:   checkDetail,\n\t\t\tDiagnosticIDs: diagnostics,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Drive built candidates !ok {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tprofiles := checks.Profiles()\n\tif len(profiles) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tround := 0\n\tif reports := state.Identities[reportIdentity(productID, 1)]; reports != nil {\n\t\tround = len(reports.Order)\n\t}\n\tstepID := fmt.Sprintf(\"verify-link/%s/v%d-%s/r%d\", productID, candidate.Seq, candidate.ContentHash[:16], round)\n\tif planned[stepID] {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tplanned[stepID] = true\n\t*steps = append(*steps, planStep{\n\t\tStepID: stepID, PassID: \"verification\", Subject: productID,\n\t\tSubjectVersion: candidate.Seq, SubjectHash: candidate.ContentHash,\n\t\tReason: \"exact link-check evidence for the staged Product candidate\",\n\t\tSignal: 1, Profile: profiles[0].ProfileID, CheckIDs: append([]string(nil), checkIDs...), ProductTree: true,\n\t})\n}\n\n// batchIntegrated reports whether every change-set in the batch is bound into\n// the current product lineage by an application_record naming the product head.\nfunc (s *Session) batchIntegrated(state *State, request *RequestState, batch []string, productID string) bool {\n\tproductHead := state.HeadVersion(productID)\n\tif productHead == nil || productHead.Stale {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfor _, csID := range batch {\n\t\tcsHead := state.HeadVersion(csID)\n\t\tif csHead == nil {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !s.applicationBound(state, request, csID, csHead, productID) {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// packetCheckRefusal reports whether a change-set candidate's latest\n// packet-checks gate result refused and preserves that exact result's detail.\n// A build output the trusted core rejected (bad packet pin, out-of-scope\n// write, incoherent base) never head-moves; the frontier re-dispatches a fresh\n// build with the mechanical refusal as its objective steer.\nfunc packetCheckRefusal(state *State, identity string, candidate *VersionState) (string, bool) {\n\tresult := latestResult(state.GateResults[gateKey{Identity: identity, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: \"packet-checks\"}])\n\tif result == nil || result.Outcome == \"pass\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\treturn result.Detail, true\n}\n\n// quarantinedPackets returns the packet ids whose build attempt budget is\n// exhausted (D0010.C9): the frontier proceeds around them, but the work-graph\n// is not fully built while any packet is quarantined. A quarantine is a\n// computed planning predicate over recorded build attempts, not stored state.\nfunc (s *Session) quarantinedPackets(state *State, request *RequestState, wg workgraph.WorkGraph) map[string]bool {\n\texcluded := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor _, p := range wg.Packets {\n\t\tcsID := packetChangeSetIdentity(request.Subject, p.ID)\n\t\tif state.HeadVersion(csID) != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue // already built \u2014 never quarantined\n\t\t}\n\t\tattempts := 0\n\t\tfor _, run := range state.Runs {\n\t\t\tif run.StepID == \"build/\"+csID && run.RequestRef == request.Seq && !run.Open() {\n\t\t\t\tattempts++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif attempts > s.Policy.RedispatchBound {\n\t\t\texcluded[p.ID] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn excluded\n}\n\n// ancestorConflictError marks a packet whose composed base cannot fold an\n// ancestor's admitted change-set \u2014 usually the tree moved under the\n// ancestor's base pin. It is this packet's articulate wait, never a session\n// error: dispatch defers the step and the session continues; the ancestor's\n// rebase-style rebuild is the planner's clause-4 move\n// (stalls-need-no-judgment@3), and the fold succeeds once the rebuilt\n// ancestor admits.\ntype ancestorConflictError struct {\n\tPacket   string\n\tAncestor string\n\tDetail   string\n}\n\nfunc (e *ancestorConflictError) Error() string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"driver: composing base for %s: ancestor %s conflicts: %s\", e.Packet, e.Ancestor, e.Detail)\n}\n\n// composedBase memoizes one packet's composed base within a single\n// composePacketBase call, so a diamond (a descendant reached through several\n// ancestor paths) folds each shared ancestor's base once, not once per path.\ntype composedBase struct {\n\tbody []byte\n\thash string\n\terr  error\n}\n\n// composePacketBase composes the base tree a packet builds against (D0010.C5):\n// the observed product tree (T_obs, empty before first integration) folded\n// with the admitted change-sets of the packet's ancestors, in Work Graph index\n// order. It returns the canonical base body and its hash.\nfunc (s *Session) composePacketBase(state *State, subject string, wg workgraph.WorkGraph, packetID string) ([]byte, string, error) {\n\treturn s.composePacketBaseMemo(state, subject, wg, packetID, map[string]composedBase{})\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) packetBaseComposition(state *State, subject string, wg workgraph.WorkGraph, packetID, resultingBaseHash string) (*changeset.BaseComposition, error) {\n\tproductID := productIdentity(subject)\n\tobserved, err := s.observedProductPin(state, subject)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tindex, err := workgraph.ApplicationIndex(wg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tpositions := make(map[string]int64, len(index))\n\tfor position, anchor := range index {\n\t\tpositions[anchor] = int64(position)\n\t}\n\tancestors := make([]changeset.IndexedChangeSetPin, 0)\n\tfor _, ancestor := range workgraph.Ancestors(wg, packetID) {\n\t\tidentity := packetChangeSetIdentity(subject, ancestor)\n\t\thead := state.HeadVersion(identity)\n\t\tif head == nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: packet %s ancestor %s has no admitted head\", packetID, ancestor)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.applicationBound(state, nil, identity, head, productID) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tancestors = append(ancestors, changeset.IndexedChangeSetPin{\n\t\t\tApplicationIndex: positions[ancestor],\n\t\t\tChangeSet:        changeset.ArtifactPin{Identity: identity, VersionSeq: head.Seq, ContentHash: head.ContentHash},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn &changeset.BaseComposition{ObservedProduct: observed, Ancestors: ancestors, ResultingBaseHash: resultingBaseHash}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) composePacketBaseMemo(state *State, subject string, wg workgraph.WorkGraph, packetID string, memo map[string]composedBase) ([]byte, string, error) {\n\tif cached, ok := memo[packetID]; ok {\n\t\treturn cached.body, cached.hash, cached.err\n\t}\n\tbody, hash, err := s.composePacketBaseFold(state, subject, wg, packetID, memo)\n\tmemo[packetID] = composedBase{body: body, hash: hash, err: err}\n\treturn body, hash, err\n}\n\n// observedProductBase resolves the exact observed Product tree a base\n// composition folds ancestors onto (D0010.C5): the subject's accepted\n// Product head, or \u2014 before first integration \u2014 the git-anchored repository\n// tree (product-lineage-anchored@1; the empty tree where no anchor\n// resolves). Packet-grain (composePacketBaseFold) and subject-grain\n// (composeSubjectAssemblyBase) base folds share this one resolution so the\n// two grains can never observe a different Product.\nfunc (s *Session) observedProductBase(state *State, subject string) (changeset.Product, error) {\n\tproductID := productIdentity(subject)\n\tvar baseBody []byte\n\tif head := state.HeadVersion(productID); head != nil {\n\t\tbody, err := s.getObject(head.ContentHash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tbaseBody = body\n\t}\n\tbase, err := changeset.ParseProduct(baseBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, err\n\t}\n\tif baseBody == nil {\n\t\t// No product lineage yet: the base is the repository tree at git\n\t\t// HEAD, as an anchored body (product-lineage-anchored@1) \u2014 real\n\t\t// bases for in-place repo edits; the lineage itself is born at\n\t\t// first integration. A planning-side world read, like observation.\n\t\t// A working tree without a git HEAD (fixtures, pre-genesis trees)\n\t\t// keeps the empty-tree base; the choice is auditable in the base\n\t\t// pin every change-set carries.\n\t\tif anchored, err := gitanchor.HeadProduct(s.Graph.RepoRoot); err == nil {\n\t\t\tbase = anchored\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn base, nil\n}\n\n// observedProductPin resolves the exact observed Product occurrence a base\n// composition pins (D0010.C5): the accepted Product head's own pin, or \u2014\n// before first integration \u2014 the content hash of observedProductBase's\n// resolved tree (VersionSeq zero marks the lawful pre-lineage absence,\n// changeset.ArtifactPin's documented convention).\nfunc (s *Session) observedProductPin(state *State, subject string) (changeset.ArtifactPin, error) {\n\tproductID := productIdentity(subject)\n\tobserved := changeset.ArtifactPin{Identity: productID}\n\tif head := state.HeadVersion(productID); head != nil {\n\t\tobserved.VersionSeq = head.Seq\n\t\tobserved.ContentHash = head.ContentHash\n\t\treturn observed, nil\n\t}\n\tbase, err := s.observedProductBase(state, subject)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn changeset.ArtifactPin{}, err\n\t}\n\thash, err := changeset.TreeHash(base)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn changeset.ArtifactPin{}, err\n\t}\n\tobserved.ContentHash = hash\n\treturn observed, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) composePacketBaseFold(state *State, subject string, wg workgraph.WorkGraph, packetID string, memo map[string]composedBase) ([]byte, string, error) {\n\tbase, err := s.observedProductBase(state, subject)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, \"\", err\n\t}\n\t// Fold the packet's ancestors, in Work Graph index order, as overlays onto\n\t// the observed product tree. Each ancestor is checked FRESH against its OWN\n\t// composed base (the product folded with the ancestor's own ancestors), not\n\t// the running cumulative tree: two independent leaves both correctly pinning\n\t// the product then compose cleanly (their write scopes are disjoint by\n\t// work-graph legality), where the old strict-Apply chain demanded the second\n\t// leaf pin the first and deadlocked every diamond-shaped graph. A genuinely\n\t// tree-moved ancestor \u2014 one whose pin no longer equals its recomputed\n\t// composed base \u2014 still surfaces a typed ancestorConflictError, the wait\n\t// clause-4's rebase-style rebuild clears.\n\tfor _, ancestor := range workgraph.Ancestors(wg, packetID) {\n\t\thead := state.HeadVersion(packetChangeSetIdentity(subject, ancestor))\n\t\tif head == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue // not yet admitted \u2014 the frontier would not have dispatched\n\t\t}\n\t\t// An ancestor whose change-set content is application-bound into the\n\t\t// current product lineage is already in the base this fold started\n\t\t// from (the product head): its overlay is a no-op, and its base pin\n\t\t// necessarily predates the integration that absorbed it. Re-checking\n\t\t// that stale pin here deadlocks every descendant \u2014 the packet loop's\n\t\t// applicationBound guard means clause-4 will never plan the rebuild\n\t\t// this conflict asks for (observed 2026-07-10: subject-grain and\n\t\t// packet lanes built byte-identical change-sets, the integration\n\t\t// bound the shared content hash, and alc-anchored-base-typed-guard\n\t\t// waited forever on its \"done\" ancestor).\n\t\tif s.applicationBound(state, nil, packetChangeSetIdentity(subject, ancestor), head, productIdentity(subject)) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tancBody, err := s.getObject(head.ContentHash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, \"\", err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(ancBody)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, \"\", err\n\t\t}\n\t\t_, ancestorBaseHash, err := s.composePacketBaseMemo(state, subject, wg, ancestor, memo)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, \"\", err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif cs.BaseHash() != ancestorBaseHash {\n\t\t\treturn nil, \"\", &ancestorConflictError{Packet: packetID, Ancestor: ancestor,\n\t\t\t\tDetail: fmt.Sprintf(\"ancestor change-set pins base %s, its composed base is %s (tree moved: rebase-style revision required; pin the full hash)\", cs.BaseHash(), ancestorBaseHash)}\n\t\t}\n\t\tnext, conflicts := changeset.ApplyOverlay(base, cs)\n\t\tif len(conflicts) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn nil, \"\", &ancestorConflictError{Packet: packetID, Ancestor: ancestor, Detail: conflicts[0].Detail}\n\t\t}\n\t\tbase = next\n\t}\n\tcanonical, err := changeset.CanonicalProduct(base)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, \"\", err\n\t}\n\thash, err := changeset.TreeHash(base)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, \"\", err\n\t}\n\treturn canonical, hash, nil\n}\n\n// changeSetPacketDisposition classifies a change-set candidate for the\n// packet-checks gate: \"strict\" for a build-flow output (it pins a work-graph\n// packet), \"default\" for a cleanly-parsed single-change-set (the E4 grain,\n// no packet pin) which the default mechanical gate governs, and \"fail\" for a\n// body that is not a well-formed change-set at all. A body that does not parse\n// must never reach the lenient default gate \u2014 that would let a lane admit\n// garbage as an accepted head, the one thing execution-free admission exists\n// to prevent (D0010.C1).\nfunc (s *Session) changeSetPacketDisposition(candidate *VersionState) string {\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(candidate.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\"\n\t}\n\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\"\n\t}\n\tif cs.Packet.PacketID != \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"strict\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"default\"\n}\n\n// evaluateChangeSetAnchoredBase refuses a change set that claims the empty tree\n// as its base while the tree it edits is non-empty. An empty base pin does not\n// mean \"I add files to what is there\" \u2014 it means \"the composed base is nothing\",\n// so integration links the change set's own files AS the entire Product.\n//\n// Live 2026-07-23: the subject-grain build for stalls-need-no-judgment-c was\n// dispatched packetless, the packetless prompt instructed the lane to leave\n// every base field empty, and the lane obligingly produced a self-contained\n// 35-file tree (adding its own go.mod so `go build ./...` would pass over it).\n// It earned two independent verification signals, passed integration-checks,\n// and was linked as product c8a02131 \u2014 a \"delivery\" containing no\n// internal/driver at all. A change set that deletes the compiler cleared every\n// gate, because no predicate asked whether the base it composed onto was the\n// repository. This is that predicate.\n//\n// The rule is narrow: it fires only on an EMPTY base pin, and only when a\n// non-empty base is resolvable \u2014 an accepted Product head for the subject, or\n// the git-anchored repository tree. A genuinely first change set over a\n// genuinely empty tree stays lawful.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluateChangeSetAnchoredBase(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) (string, string) {\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(candidate.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set body unreadable: \" + err.Error()\n\t}\n\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif cs.BaseHash() != \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\"\n\t}\n\tsubject := strings.TrimSuffix(identity.ID, \"/change-set\")\n\tproductID := productIdentity(subject)\n\tif head := state.HeadVersion(productID); head != nil && !head.Stale {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\n\t\t\t\"change-set declares an empty base while %s head %d (%.16s) is the composed base: an empty base pin links this change-set's files as the whole Product\",\n\t\t\tproductID, head.Seq, head.ContentHash)\n\t}\n\tcommits, err := gitanchor.RecentCommits(s.Graph.RepoRoot, 1)\n\tif err != nil || len(commits) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\"\n\t}\n\tanchor, err := gitanchor.AnchorAtCommit(s.Graph.RepoRoot, commits[0])\n\tif err != nil || anchor == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\"\n\t}\n\tanchored := changeset.Product{SchemaVersion: 1, Files: map[string]string{}, Anchor: anchor}\n\tanchoredHash, err := changeset.TreeHash(anchored)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\n\t\t\"change-set declares an empty base while the anchored repository tree %.16s (commit %.12s) is the composed base: an empty base pin links this change-set's files as the whole Product\",\n\t\tanchoredHash, commits[0])\n}\n\n// evaluateChangeSetResultTree is the grain-independent half of packet-checks:\n// the candidate declares a result tree, and applying its own overlay to its own\n// pinned base yields exactly that tree. It returns (\"\", \"\") when the predicate\n// holds so the caller continues to the gate it was already running.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluateChangeSetResultTree(state *State, candidate *VersionState) (string, string) {\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(candidate.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set body unreadable: \" + err.Error()\n\t}\n\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif cs.ResultTreeHash == \"\" {\n\t\t// A v1 body that declares no result tree makes no claim to falsify:\n\t\t// Apply skips the comparison and every downstream composition derives\n\t\t// the tree instead of checking it. Silence is not a lie, and refusing\n\t\t// it here would retire the historical single-change-set shape rather\n\t\t// than the mis-declaration this gate exists to catch.\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\"\n\t}\n\tderived, err := s.deriveResultTreeHash(state, body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set result tree does not derive: \" + err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif derived != cs.ResultTreeHash {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"change-set result tree %s differs from derived tree %s\", cs.ResultTreeHash, derived)\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", \"\"\n}\n\n// evaluatePacketChecks is the packet-checks gate predicate (RFC 0003,\n// D0010.C4, execution-free): the change-set pins the accepted work-graph\n// packet element, its diff stays within the packet's declared write scope, its\n// base is the composed base the driver computes, and it was produced by a build\n// run. Buildability itself lives in verification, never here.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluatePacketChecks(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) (string, string) {\n\trun, ok := state.Runs[candidate.ProducedByRun]\n\tif !ok || run.PassID != \"build\" {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set was not produced by a build run\"\n\t}\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(candidate.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set body unreadable: \" + err.Error()\n\t}\n\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif cs.SchemaVersion != 2 || cs.BaseComposition == nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"legacy Change Set has no exact packet/base-composition force\"\n\t}\n\tif cs.Packet.PacketID == \"\" || cs.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set pins no work-graph packet\"\n\t}\n\twgHead := state.HeadVersion(cs.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity)\n\tif wgHead == nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set pins work-graph with no accepted head\"\n\t}\n\twgBody, err := s.getObject(wgHead.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"work-graph body unreadable: \" + err.Error()\n\t}\n\twg, err := workgraph.Parse(wgBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tpacket, ok := wg.Packet(cs.Packet.PacketID)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"packet %q not present in the pinned work-graph\", cs.Packet.PacketID)\n\t}\n\tif err := changeset.ValidatePacketPin(cs.Packet, cs.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity, wg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif _, err := s.getObject(cs.Packet.WorkGraphHash); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set origin work-graph version is unavailable\"\n\t}\n\tmanifestPacket, _ := run.Manifest[\"packet_pin\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !mapsEqual(manifestPacket, packetManifestPinValue(cs.Packet)) {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set packet pin differs from its sealed build Run Manifest\"\n\t}\n\t// acceptance-checks-bind@1, legacy defense: a change-set built from a\n\t// work-graph admitted before legality enforced resolution still refuses\n\t// articulately here rather than verifying hollow.\n\tif len(packet.AcceptanceChecks) > 0 {\n\t\tif s.Checks == nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"packet %q names acceptance checks but no check registry is loaded (acceptance_check_unresolvable)\", packet.ID)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, unresolvable := s.Checks.Registry.ResolveEntries(packet.AcceptanceChecks); len(unresolvable) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"packet %q names acceptance checks that resolve to no registry set or check id: %s (acceptance_check_unresolvable)\", packet.ID, strings.Join(unresolvable, \", \"))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tplanned, _ := run.Manifest[\"planned_step\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif boolOf(planned, \"target_check_authority_bridge\") {\n\t\tif s.Checks == nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge has no loaded Check registry\"\n\t\t}\n\t\trequest := state.Requests[run.RequestRef]\n\t\tif request == nil || request.TargetProjection == nil ||\n\t\t\trequest.TargetProjection.Evaluation.SubjectRole != \"product\" ||\n\t\t\trequest.TargetProjection.Evaluation.Materialization != \"product_tree\" ||\n\t\t\tidentity.ID != subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"change-set\") {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge has no exact Product evaluator or subject Change Set context\"\n\t\t}\n\t\trequired := s.Checks.Registry.Set(request.TargetProjection.Evaluation.CheckSetID)\n\t\tauthorized, unresolvable := s.Checks.Registry.ResolveEntries(packet.AcceptanceChecks)\n\t\tif len(required) == 0 || len(unresolvable) != 0 || !requiredChecksAuthorized(authorized, required) {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge packet does not carry the exact evaluator Check authority\"\n\t\t}\n\t\toriginHash := str(planned, \"target_check_authority_origin_graph\")\n\t\toriginBody, err := s.getObject(originHash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge has no exact pre-bridge Work Graph\"\n\t\t}\n\t\torigin, err := workgraph.Parse(originBody)\n\t\tif err != nil || origin.SchemaVersion != 2 {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge pre-bridge Work Graph is malformed\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, existed := origin.Packet(packet.ID); existed {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge packet was not newly added after the sealed pre-bridge Work Graph\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(packet.DependsOn) != 0 {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge packet must be dependency-free\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(cs.Files) != 0 || len(cs.Deletes) != 0 {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"target Check authority bridge Change Set must be a no-op over the current Product base\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// Write-scope conformance: every written or deleted path is in scope.\n\tfor path := range cs.Files {\n\t\tif !workgraph.InScope(path, packet.WriteScope) {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"write %q is outside packet %q write scope %v\", path, packet.ID, packet.WriteScope)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfor _, path := range cs.Deletes {\n\t\tif !workgraph.InScope(path, packet.WriteScope) {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"delete %q is outside packet %q write scope %v\", path, packet.ID, packet.WriteScope)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// Base coherence: the change-set's base is the composed base for the packet.\n\tsubject := strings.TrimSuffix(cs.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity, \"/work-graph\")\n\t_, baseHash, err := s.composePacketBase(state, subject, wg, cs.Packet.PacketID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\twantComposition, err := s.packetBaseComposition(state, subject, wg, cs.Packet.PacketID, baseHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif !reflect.DeepEqual(cs.BaseComposition, wantComposition) {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set base composition differs from the exact current observed Product/ancestor set\"\n\t}\n\tmanifestBase, _ := run.Manifest[\"base_composition\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !mapsEqual(manifestBase, baseCompositionValue(*cs.BaseComposition)) {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set base composition differs from its sealed build Run Manifest\"\n\t}\n\tif derived, err := s.deriveResultTreeHash(state, body); err != nil || derived != cs.ResultTreeHash {\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set result tree does not derive: \" + err.Error()\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"change-set result tree %s differs from derived tree %s\", cs.ResultTreeHash, derived)\n\t}\n\treturn \"pass\", \"\"\n}\n\n// evaluateWorkGraphLegality is the work-graph-legality gate predicate\n// (RFC 0003, D0010.C3, execution-free): the candidate work-graph is acyclic,\n// every packet carries a derived_from plan-element anchor, parallel packets\n// declare disjoint write scopes, the index is the canonical topological order\n// (re-runs are identity-preserving), and the graph derives from the accepted,\n// fresh Implementation Plan it pins.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluateWorkGraphLegality(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) (string, string) {\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(candidate.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"work-graph body unreadable: \" + err.Error()\n\t}\n\twg, err := workgraph.Parse(body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif findings := workgraph.Legal(wg); len(findings) > 0 {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"work-graph illegal: %s (%s)\", findings[0].Detail, findings[0].Class)\n\t}\n\t// The graph must derive from the accepted, fresh plan head it pins.\n\tplanHead := state.HeadVersion(wg.Plan.Identity)\n\tif planHead == nil {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"work-graph pins plan %q which has no accepted head\", wg.Plan.Identity)\n\t}\n\tif planHead.Stale {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"work-graph derives from a stale plan head %q \u2014 revalidate before lowering\", wg.Plan.Identity)\n\t}\n\tif planHead.ContentHash != wg.Plan.ContentHash {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"work-graph pins plan %.16s, accepted plan head is %.16s\", wg.Plan.ContentHash, planHead.ContentHash)\n\t}\n\tif wg.SchemaVersion != 2 || wg.Plan.VersionSeq != planHead.Seq {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"work-graph does not pin the exact accepted plan occurrence\"\n\t}\n\tif identity != nil && identity.Head != 0 && identity.Head != candidate.Seq {\n\t\tprior := identity.Versions[identity.Head]\n\t\tif prior != nil {\n\t\t\tpriorBody, readErr := s.getObject(prior.ContentHash)\n\t\t\tpriorGraph, parseErr := workgraph.Parse(priorBody)\n\t\t\tif readErr == nil && parseErr == nil && priorGraph.SchemaVersion == 2 {\n\t\t\t\tretired := make([]workgraph.RetiredPacket, len(candidate.RetiredAnchors))\n\t\t\t\tfor index, entry := range candidate.RetiredAnchors {\n\t\t\t\t\tretired[index] = workgraph.RetiredPacket{Anchor: entry.Anchor, Reason: entry.Reason}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tclaimed := s.claimedWorkPackets(state, identity.ID, priorGraph)\n\t\t\t\tif findings := workgraph.ValidateContinuity(priorGraph, wg, retired, claimed, s.authorizedContinuityAnchors(state, priorGraph, wg)); len(findings) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"work-graph continuity refused: %s (%s)\", findings[0].Detail, findings[0].Class)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// acceptance-checks-bind@1: every acceptance_checks entry resolves in the\n\t// check registry (set name or check id) \u2014 a work-graph naming a check\n\t// nothing will run is refused before it is lowered, never silently\n\t// unverified (the RQ-7555 hollow-Verified counterexample).\n\tfor _, packet := range wg.Packets {\n\t\tif len(packet.AcceptanceChecks) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.Checks == nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"packet %q names acceptance checks but no check registry is loaded (acceptance_check_unresolvable)\", packet.ID)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, unresolvable := s.Checks.Registry.ResolveEntries(packet.AcceptanceChecks); len(unresolvable) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", fmt.Sprintf(\"packet %q names acceptance checks that resolve to no registry set or check id: %s (acceptance_check_unresolvable)\", packet.ID, strings.Join(unresolvable, \", \"))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"pass\", \"\"\n}\n\n// authorizedContinuityAnchors derives the bounded anchor set whose claimed\n// movement or retirement carries exact Plan authority (RFC 0010 amendment,\n// STRNEX-36; Principal ruling 2026-07-19, bracket RQ-66713): the prior\n// graph's Plan occurrence was personally rejected at its acceptance gate,\n// the candidate graph's Plan occurrence is the personally accepted successor\n// of that exact occurrence, and the anchor's source Plan element changed or\n// vanished across the lineage. Graduated/mechanical (D0016) acceptance\n// carries no restructuring force. ValidateContinuity stays pure; every\n// ledger read lives here.\nfunc (s *Session) authorizedContinuityAnchors(state *State, prior, candidate workgraph.WorkGraph) map[string]bool {\n\treturn s.authorizedContinuityAnchorsAt(state, prior, candidate, ^uint64(0))\n}\n\n// authorizedContinuityAnchorsAt is the historical-cut form used when later\n// planning must prove why an already-accepted Work Graph was allowed to move\n// a claimed packet. A later withdrawal of the successor Plan cannot\n// retroactively erase authority that the packetization gate observed at its\n// immutable cut; it can only invalidate descendants prospectively.\nfunc (s *Session) authorizedContinuityAnchorsAt(state *State, prior, candidate workgraph.WorkGraph, cut uint64) map[string]bool {\n\tplanID := candidate.Plan.Identity\n\tif planID == \"\" || prior.Plan.Identity != planID {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tpriorSeq, nextSeq := prior.Plan.VersionSeq, candidate.Plan.VersionSeq\n\tif priorSeq == 0 || nextSeq <= priorSeq {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tidentity, ok := state.Identities[planID]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tpriorPlan, nextPlan := identity.Versions[priorSeq], identity.Versions[nextSeq]\n\tif priorPlan == nil || nextPlan == nil ||\n\t\tpriorPlan.ContentHash != prior.Plan.ContentHash || nextPlan.ContentHash != candidate.Plan.ContentHash {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif !s.personalAcceptanceVerdictAt(state, identity, priorPlan, false, cut) ||\n\t\t!s.personalAcceptanceVerdictAt(state, identity, nextPlan, true, cut) {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tauthorized := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor _, packet := range prior.Packets {\n\t\tanchor := packet.DerivedFrom\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(anchor, \"#el:\") {\n\t\t\tanchor = strings.TrimPrefix(anchor, \"#\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tanchor = strings.TrimPrefix(anchor, \"el:\")\n\t\tif anchor == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif priorPlan.Anchors[anchor] != nextPlan.Anchors[anchor] {\n\t\t\tauthorized[packet.ID] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn authorized\n}\n\n// personalAcceptanceVerdict reports whether the version's latest\n// acceptance-class verdict has the wanted outcome under personal Principal\n// authority: authority.kind is principal and no authority_proof is pinned.\n// The D0016 graduated path pins its condition proof, and per the Principal's\n// 2026-07-19 ruling it carries no claimed-packet restructuring force; a\n// personal acceptance wielded under standing delegation does.\nfunc (s *Session) personalAcceptanceVerdict(state *State, identity *IdentityState, version *VersionState, accepted bool) bool {\n\treturn s.personalAcceptanceVerdictAt(state, identity, version, accepted, ^uint64(0))\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) personalAcceptanceVerdictAt(state *State, identity *IdentityState, version *VersionState, accepted bool, cut uint64) bool {\n\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[version.Kind]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tif s.Catalog.Gates[gateID].Class != \"acceptance\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: version.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tvar latest *GateResultState\n\t\tfor index := range state.GateResults[key] {\n\t\t\tresult := &state.GateResults[key][index]\n\t\t\tif result.Seq <= cut && (latest == nil || result.Seq > latest.Seq) {\n\t\t\t\tlatest = result\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif latest == nil {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tif accepted != (latest.Outcome == \"pass\") {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tauthority, _ := latest.Payload[\"authority\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\tif str(authority, \"kind\") != \"principal\" {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tif proof, present := authority[\"authority_proof\"]; present && proof != nil {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) claimedWorkPackets(state *State, workGraphIdentity string, graph workgraph.WorkGraph) map[string]bool {\n\tclaimed := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor _, identity := range state.Identities {\n\t\tif identity.Kind != \"change-set\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, seq := range identity.Order {\n\t\t\tversion := identity.Versions[seq]\n\t\t\tbody, err := s.getObject(version.ContentHash)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tchange, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\t\t\tif err != nil || change.SchemaVersion != 2 || change.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity != workGraphIdentity {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\twant, err := workgraph.PacketElementHash(graph, change.Packet.PacketID)\n\t\t\tif err == nil && want == change.Packet.PacketElementHash {\n\t\t\t\tclaimed[change.Packet.PacketID] = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// An open run claims its packet before any matching Change Set is\n\t// admitted (STRNEX-36 step 8): in-flight work is as strandable as\n\t// admitted work, so continuity protects it the same way.\n\tfor _, run := range state.Runs {\n\t\tif !run.Open() || run.Manifest == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tstep, _ := run.Manifest[\"planned_step\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\tpacket := str(step, \"packet\")\n\t\tif packet == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, input := range stringsOf(step, \"inputs\") {\n\t\t\tif input == workGraphIdentity {\n\t\t\t\tclaimed[packet] = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn claimed\n}\n\nfunc packetManifestPinValue(pin changeset.PacketPin) map[string]any {\n\treturn map[string]any{\n\t\t\"work_graph_identity\": pin.WorkGraphIdentity, \"work_graph_version_hash\": pin.WorkGraphHash,\n\t\t\"packet_anchor\": pin.PacketID, \"packet_element_hash\": pin.PacketElementHash,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc baseCompositionValue(composition changeset.BaseComposition) map[string]any {\n\tancestors := make([]any, len(composition.Ancestors))\n\tfor index, ancestor := range composition.Ancestors {\n\t\tancestors[index] = map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"application_index\": ancestor.ApplicationIndex,\n\t\t\t\"change_set\":        artifactPinValue(ancestor.ChangeSet),\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn map[string]any{\n\t\t\"observed_product\": map[string]any {\n\treturn map[string]any{\"identity\": pin.Identity, \"version_seq\": int64(pin.VersionSeq), \"content_hash\": pin.ContentHash}\n}\n\n// planTreeMovedRebuild is clause 4's typed dispatch (stalls-need-no-judgment@3,\n// D0010.C5/C8 via internal/planner): when an accepted change-set head's pinned\n// base no longer equals the current composed base, moved=true and the returned\n// step (nil once the round budget caps and attempts accumulate toward the\n// existing escalation) is a bounded rebase-style build revision against the\n// current composed base, with the doomed head pinned as diagnostic. Scope is\n// exactly the recompose-mismatch (tree_moved) shape; unreadable bodies,\n// packetless grains, and uncomposable bases are not this dispatch's to judge.\nfunc (s *Session) planTreeMovedRebuild(state *State, request *RequestState, csIdentity string, head *VersionState, planned map[string]bool) (*planStep, bool) {\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(head.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\tif err != nil || cs.Packet.PacketID == \"\" || cs.Packet.WorkGraphHash == \"\" || cs.BaseHash() == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\twgBody, err := s.getObject(cs.Packet.WorkGraphHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\twg, err := workgraph.Parse(wgBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\t_, baseHash, err := s.composePacketBase(state, request.Subject, wg, cs.Packet.PacketID)\n\tif err != nil || baseHash == cs.BaseHash() {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tsubject := ConflictClass: planner.TreeMoved},\n\t}\n\tif planner.Reintegrate(subject, head.ContentHash) == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tplan := planner.PlanRecovery(subject, s.Policy.RevisionBound)\n\tif plan.Kind != planner.PlanRebuildRevision {\n\t\treturn nil, true\n\t}\n\tround := 0\n\tif identityState, ok := state.Identities[csIdentity]; ok {\n\t\tround = len(identityState.Order)\n\t}\n\tif round > s.Policy.RevisionBound {\n\t\tround = s.Policy.RevisionBound\n\t}\n\theadPin := store.Pin{Identity: csIdentity, VersionSeq: head.Seq, ContentHash: head.ContentHash}\n\tdiagnostic := state.exactTreeMovedDiagnostic(headPin)\n\tstepID := fmt.Sprintf(\"revise/%s/%d-r%d\", csIdentity, head.Seq, round)\n\tvar recoveryContext map[string]any\n\tcheckDetail := \"\"\n\tif diagnostic != nil {\n\t\tstepID = fmt.Sprintf(\"revise/%s/%d-tree-%s-r%d\", csIdentity, head.Seq, diagnostic.OperationKey[:16], round)\n\t\trecoveryContext = map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"kind\": \"tree_moved\", driverPinValue(headPin),\n\t\t\t\"expected_base\": clonePayload(diagnostic.ExpectedBase), \"current_base\": clonePayload(diagnostic.CurrentBase),\n\t\t}\n\t\tcheckDetail = diagnostic.Detail\n\t}\n\tif planned[stepID] {\n\t\treturn nil, true\n\t}\n\tplanned[stepID] = true\n\twgIdentity := cs.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity\n\tif wgIdentity == \"\" {\n\t\twgIdentity = subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"work-graph\")\n\t}\n\treturn &planStep{\n\t\tStepID:                          stepID,\n\t\tPassID:                          \"build\",\n\t\tSubject:                         csIdentity,\n\t\tInputs:                          []string{wgIdentity},\n\t\tPacket:                          cs.Packet.PacketID,\n\t\tReason:                          plan.Reason,\n\t\tDiagnosticIDs:                   []string{csIdentity},\n\t\tRecoveryContext:                 recoveryContext,\n\t\tCheckDetail:                     checkDetail,\n\t\tTargetCheckAuthorityBridge:      targetCheckAuthorityBridgeVersion(state, head),\n\t\tTargetCheckAuthorityOriginGraph: targetCheckAuthorityOriginGraph(state, head),\n\t}, true\n}\n\n// planVerificationRejectRevision routes a conclusively-rejected change-set head\n// to a bounded build revision instead of a futile re-verification. The build\n// frontier already does this for packet change-sets (planWorkGraphBuild via\n// verificationRejected); this is the same wiring for a subject-level change-set\n// verified through the verified_dual_signal conjunct. A deterministic mechanical\n// NO from the teeth (go vet/test) is not a flake: re-minting the same verify\n// objective only re-runs the same deterministic check to the same reject until\n// the redispatch bound escalates (observed: cap-b's subject change-set looped\n// verify/signal-2 to round 14 and exhausted, never revising). The lawful\n// successor is a fresh build that reads the rejecting report as a declared\n// diagnostic and, rebased on the current composed base, produces content the\n// teeth accept \u2014 reject-verdict\u2192revision (stalls-need-no-judgment@4). This also\n// cures the tree-moved base-dependent reject: the rebuild composes against the\n// current base, so a change-set that fails only against a since-moved base is\n// re-produced consistent with the tree it will be re-verified on. Returns the\n// revision step, or nil when the head is not conclusively rejected or a revision\n// for it is already planned.\nfunc (s *Session) planVerificationRejectRevision(state *State, identity string, head *VersionState, planned map[string]bool) *planStep {\n\treportID, rejected := s.verificationRejected(state, identity, head)\n\tif !rejected {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tstepID := \"revise/\" + identity + \"/\" + fmt.Sprint(head.Seq)\n\tif planned[stepID] {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tdiagnostics := []string{identity}\n\tif reportID != \"\" {\n\t\tdiagnostics = append(diagnostics, reportID)\n\t}\n\tstep := planStep{\n\t\tStepID:                          stepID,\n\t\tPassID:                          \"build\",\n\t\tSubject:                         identity,\n\t\tReason:                          \"verification rejected the change-set head: revision (a deterministic reject routes to a bounded rebuild, not re-verification)\",\n\t\tDiagnosticIDs:                   diagnostics,\n\t\tTargetCheckAuthorityBridge:      targetCheckAuthorityBridgeVersion(state, head),\n\t\tTargetCheckAuthorityOriginGraph: targetCheckAuthorityOriginGraph(state, head),\n\t}\n\t// A build-flow change-set pins the work-graph packet it implements; its\n\t// revision rebuilds against that packet's composed base. Read the pin from\n\t// the immutable head body. A change-set with no packet pin revises as a\n\t// plain build against the product/empty base (no Packet/Inputs).\n\tif body, err := s.getObject(head.ContentHash); err == nil {\n\t\tif cs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body); err == nil && cs.Packet.PacketID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tstep.Packet = cs.Packet.PacketID\n\t\t\twgIdentity := cs.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity\n\t\t\tif wgIdentity == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\twgIdentity = subjectIdentity(strings.TrimSuffix(identity, \"/change-set\"), \"work-graph\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstep.Inputs = []string{wgIdentity}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tplanned[stepID] = true\n\treturn &step\n}\n\n// changeSetDeclaredVersusDerivedTree returns the tree a change-set declares and\n// the tree its own overlay actually produces over its own pinned base. The\n// derivation runs with the declared hash stripped from the body, so Apply's\n// self-check does not turn the very mismatch being measured into an error.\n//\n// It reports ok=false whenever the comparison is not the one being asked for:\n// a body that declares no result tree makes no claim, and a base that does not\n// resolve (moved, absent, unlinkable) is staleness \u2014 owned by the tree-moved\n// and packet-context branches, which must keep their frontier.\nfunc (s *Session) changeSetDeclaredVersusDerivedTree(state *State, version *VersionState) (string, string, bool) {\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(version.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(body)\n\tif err != nil || cs.ResultTreeHash == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tvar wire map[string]any\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(body, &wire); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tdelete(wire, \"result_tree_hash\")\n\tstripped, err := json.Marshal(wire)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tderived, err := s.deriveResultTreeHash(state, stripped)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\treturn cs.ResultTreeHash, derived, true\n}\n\n// planUnderivableChangeSetRebuild routes an accepted Change Set head that\n// fails its own arithmetic to a bounded rebuild instead of demanding signals\n// on it forever. The defect is computable from the head's own immutable body \u2014\n// applying its overlay to its pinned base does not yield the tree it declares \u2014\n// so no ledger diagnostic is needed to see it and no execution can repair it:\n// verification cannot compose the subject, integration cannot apply it, and a\n// receipt cannot bind to a tree that never materializes. This is the same\n// clause-four shape as the tree-moved rebuild (D0010.C8), one step earlier in\n// the causal chain, and it is declared mechanical policy rather than judgment.\n//\n// Live counterexample it retires (BUG-2026-07-23): head 72075 declared\n// 827b44a1 over an empty base whose overlay yields c8d6cf8f. Every dispatch\n// refused synchronously in the adapter, the runs were reaped as orphans, and\n// the funnel re-planned the identical doomed compose until two attempt budgets\n// escalated \u2014 with the orphan's wording, not the arithmetic, as the diagnosis.\n// Heads admitted before packet-checks derived E4 arithmetic are exactly the\n// population this branch drains.\nfunc (s *Session) planUnderivableChangeSetRebuild(state *State, identity string, head *VersionState, planned map[string]bool) (*planStep, bool) {\n\tif head == nil || head.Kind != \"change-set\" {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tdeclared, derived, ok := s.changeSetDeclaredVersusDerivedTree(state, head)\n\tif !ok || declared == derived {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tdetail := fmt.Sprintf(\"change-set declares result tree %s; The pin names an\n// immutable (commit, tree): HEAD is tried first, then recent ancestors, so\n// commits landing after the build (the operator repairing the driver while\n// a funnel runs) never orphan an in-flight change-set \u2014 the base it pinned\n// still exists in git, content-addressed.\nfunc (s *Session) anchoredHeadBase(pin string) ([]byte, bool) {\n\tcommits, err := gitanchor.RecentCommits(s.Graph.RepoRoot, 50)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tfor _, commit := range commits {\n\t\tanchor, err := gitanchor.AnchorAtCommit(s.Graph.RepoRoot, commit)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tanchored := changeset.Product{SchemaVersion: 1, Files: map[string]string{}, Anchor: anchor}\n\t\thash, err := changeset.TreeHash(anchored)\n\t\tif err != nil || hash != pin {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tbody, err := changeset.CanonicalProduct(anchored)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, false\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn body, true\n\t}\n\treturn nil, false\n}\n",
    "internal/driver/session.go": "package driver\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/sha256\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"slices\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/admission\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/backend\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/changeset\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/checks\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/gate\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/gitanchor\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/records\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/scheduler\"\n\tspoolclose \"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/spool\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/store\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/workgraph\"\n)\n\n// ErrDriveLeaseBusy means another drive session holds the lease (exit 4).\nvar ErrDriveLeaseBusy = errors.New(\"driver: drive lease busy\")\n\n// Adapter is the driver-facing slice of one execution backend adapter: the\n// dispatch verb (exit-0-equivalent nil error is durable acceptance, never a\n// wait for execution) and the advisory abort verb the session issues as\n// courtesy under the winner rule. Everything else about the backend lives\n// below the seam (RFC 0007).\ntype Adapter interface {\n\tDispatch(manifestPath string) (string, error)\n\tAbort(dispatchID string) error\n}\n\n// WakeProjection is the non-authoritative liveness projection owned by RFC\n// 0006. The Session invokes it only while holding drive.lease: entry repairs\n// the standing floor, successful quiescence replaces the exact horizon, and\n// failed exits repair the floor again before releasing serialization.\ntype WakeProjection interface {\n\tEnsureFloor() error\n\tReplace(nextHorizon string) error\n}\n\n// Session executes drive sessions over one Graph Store.\ntype Session struct {\n\tGraph           *store.Graph\n\tSpool           backend.Spool\n\tAdapters        map[string]Adapter\n\tCatalog         Catalog\n\tPolicy          Policy\n\tKinds           map[string]store.ArtifactKind\n\tBackends        []scheduler.Declaration\n\tSchedulerPolicy scheduler.SchedulerPolicyPin\n\t// SchedulerBackendPreferences is the pass-scoped preference projection\n\t// parsed from the exact SchedulerPolicy body loaded for this session.\n\tSchedulerBackendPreferences map[string][]string\n\tSchedulerAliasing           scheduler.SchedulerAliasingInput\n\tChecks                      *CheckWorld\n\tEnvironment                 EnvironmentWorld\n\tFailureClasses              admission.Catalog\n\tSealKeys                    map[string][]byte\n\t// Backlog is the declared scheduling backlog (policy/backlog.yaml) the\n\t// post-quiescence schedule phase consults\n\t// (planner-auto-schedules-the-backlog@1). Read-only Principal policy\n\t// (C1): the session never mutates it and holds no path back to its file.\n\tBacklog         Backlog\n\tNow             func() time.Time\n\tInstance        string\n\tTrigger         string\n\tWakeProjection  WakeProjection\n\tmaxIterations   int\n\tabandonedInThis int\n\t// backlogIssuedInThis counts planner-scheduled requests appended during\n\t// the current drive invocation: the per-drive cap (C7) spans every\n\t// re-entry of the schedule phase within one Drive, and resets when the\n\t// next Drive begins.\n\tbacklogIssuedInThis int\n\tdispatchedInThis    map[string]bool\n\n\t// Incremental fold: the session folds the ledger once and applies only\n\t// the appended tail thereafter (fold-equivalence guarded). Derived and\n\t// destroyable; an intact segment roll preserves the cursor, while a missing,\n\t// truncated, or non-chaining boundary forces a full refold.\n\tfoldState *State\n\tfoldScan  store.TailScan\n\t// admissionRecoveryClean means the durable v2 admission recovery queues\n\t// were empty at foldScan and no relevant tail record has invalidated that\n\t// fact. Successful drives persist it with the fold snapshot, so an ordinary\n\t// wake need not rebuild the store's separate admission projections.\n\tadmissionRecoveryClean bool\n\tauthorityRecoveryClean bool\n\tstalenessRecoveryClean bool\n\t// recordSnapshot is an injected cold-fold seam used by focused tests. Nil\n\t// selects Graph.SnapshotRecords.\n\trecordSnapshot func() ([]records.DecodedRecord, store.TailScan, error)\n\n\t// expandedAnchorTrees memoizes immutable, content-addressed git-tree reads\n\t// for this Session. Product overlays are applied after lookup, so bodies\n\t// that share one pinned tree also share its single world read.\n\texpandedAnchorTrees map[string]changeset.Product\n\t// materializedProducts memoizes the immutable materialized-tree hash for an\n\t// exact Product version. Application-force revalidation may consult the\n\t// same Product hundreds of times while unrelated evidence gates advance.\n\tmaterializedProducts map[string]store.ProductRef\n\t// objectBodies bounds repeated verified reads of immutable Graph Store\n\t// bodies to this Session; store integrity and reconciliation stay below it.\n\tobjectBodies *objectReadCache\n\t// Parsed artifact bodies are immutable and content-addressed. Nested\n\t// report -> Receipt -> Change Set applicability revisits the same bodies\n\t// many times, so decode each exact body once per Session.\n\tparsedChangeSets   map[string]changeset.ChangeSet\n\tparsedReceipts     map[string]checks.Receipt\n\tparsedReports      map[string]checks.Report\n\treceiptSealChecks  map[string]error\n\treportSealChecks   map[string]error\n\treceiptEvaluations map[string]gateEvaluationMemo\n\t// envApplicability memoizes the content-pure half of semantic-environment\n\t// applicability by run manifest hash; decision revocation re-runs live on\n\t// every hit, and lock-held witnesses use the uncached variant instead.\n\tenvApplicability map[string]envApplicabilityMemo\n\t// resultTreeHashes is scoped to one progress fold. It prevents hundreds of\n\t// receipts over the same Change Set from re-composing and re-hashing the\n\t// same repository-sized tree. Each memo binds a freshly recomputed compact\n\t// fingerprint of the exact Product/ancestor/application-force composition,\n\t// rather than a fragile list of record classes that might affect it.\n\tresultTreeHashes map[string]resultTreeHashMemo\n}\n\ntype resultTreeHashMemo struct {\n\thash    string\n\tcontext string\n}\n\ntype gateEvaluationMemo struct {\n\toutcome string\n\tdetail  string\n}\n\n// Report summarizes one drive session.\ntype Report struct {\n\tQuiescence   string         `json:\"quiescence_reason\"`\n\tDrained      []string       `json:\"drained\"`\n\tActionCounts map[string]int `json:\"action_counts\"`\n\tNextHorizon  string         `json:\"next_horizon,omitempty\"`\n\tNotes        []string       `json:\"notes,omitempty\"`\n\tSessionSeq   uint64         `json:\"session_seq\"`\n}\n\n// Blocked reports whether the session ended quiescent-blocked (exit 5).\nfunc (r Report) Blocked() bool {\n\tswitch r.Quiescence {\n\tcase \"satisfied\", \"idle\":\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// Drive runs one drive session to quiescence: drain, progress, mark, plan,\n// dispatch, sweep \u2014 deterministic over (graph, ledger, catalog, policy).\nfunc (s *Session) Drive() (report Report, retErr error) {\n\treport = Report{ActionCounts: map[string]int{}}\n\tif s.Now == nil {\n\t\ts.Now = time.Now\n\t}\n\tif s.Instance == \"\" {\n\t\ts.Instance = \"drive\"\n\t}\n\tif s.Trigger == \"\" {\n\t\ts.Trigger = \"principal\"\n\t}\n\tif s.maxIterations == 0 {\n\t\ts.maxIterations = 64\n\t}\n\t// The per-drive planner cap (C7) spans every re-entry of the schedule\n\t// phase within this Drive and resets when the next Drive begins.\n\ts.backlogIssuedInThis = 0\n\ts.dispatchedInThis = map[string]bool{}\n\n\tlease, err := s.Graph.TryDriveLease()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, store.ErrLockBusy) {\n\t\t\treturn report, ErrDriveLeaseBusy\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn report, err\n\t}\n\tdefer lease.Unlock()\n\tif s.WakeProjection != nil {\n\t\trepairWake := func() {\n\t\t\tappendWakeProjectionNote(&report, s.WakeProjection.EnsureFloor())\n\t\t}\n\t\trepairWake()\n\t\tdefer func() {\n\t\t\tif retErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\trepairWake()\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tappendWakeProjectionNote(&report, s.WakeProjection.Replace(report.NextHorizon))\n\t\t}()\n\t}\n\n\t// Cheap tail check at session start.\n\tif _, err := s.Graph.RecoverActiveSegment(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn report, err\n\t}\n\tstartState, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn report, err\n\t}\n\tcursorStart := startState.LastSeq\n\n\tfor iteration := 0; ; iteration++ {\n\t\tif iteration >= s.maxIterations {\n\t\t\treturn report, errors.New(\"driver: session did not quiesce\")\n\t\t}\n\t\titerationState, err := s.fold()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\titerationStartSeq := iterationState.LastSeq\n\t\tcount := func(phase string, n int) {\n\t\t\tif n > 0 {\n\t\t\t\treport.ActionCounts[phase] += n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tn, err := s.recoverAuthorityEffects()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"authority_recovery\", n)\n\t\tn, err = s.drain(&report)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"drain\", n)\n\t\t// Durable dispatch-prefix recovery runs after canonical drain, so an\n\t\t// already-present terminal report closes its old run before any adapter\n\t\t// retry. It still precedes progress/planning and therefore can only\n\t\t// resume recorded work, never compete with newly minted work.\n\t\tif n, err = s.recoverDispatchPrefixes(&report); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"recover\", n)\n\t\tif n, err = s.progress(&report); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"progress\", n)\n\t\tif n, err = s.mark(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"mark\", n)\n\t\tif n, err = s.plan(&report); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"plan\", n)\n\t\tif n, err = s.dispatch(&report); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"dispatch\", n)\n\t\tif n, err = s.sweep(&report); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount(\"sweep\", n)\n\t\t// Only an appended provenance record is compiler progress. Adapter\n\t\t// acceptance, disposable transport repair, and idempotent recovery may\n\t\t// report work without moving graph truth; immediately re-entering every\n\t\t// compiler phase for those effects spins until maxIterations. The\n\t\t// post-work spool scan still closes the submission race, and durable\n\t\t// wakes resume on a later submission or horizon.\n\t\titerationEndState, err := s.fold()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif iterationEndState.LastSeq == iterationStartSeq {\n\t\t\t// Post-work spool re-scan closes the wake-drop race.\n\t\t\tpending, err := s.undrainedSubmissions()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif len(pending) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\t// Active advancement is quiescent for this invocation: the\n\t\t\t\t// planner-scheduling phase consults the declared backlog\n\t\t\t\t// (C2). Fresh issuance re-enters the loop so the scheduled\n\t\t\t\t// requests plan and dispatch in this same session; the next\n\t\t\t\t// quiescence's re-consultation is a no-op by construction \u2014\n\t\t\t\t// every issued entry now has an active request (C4, C10).\n\t\t\t\tscheduled, err := s.schedule()\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn report, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif scheduled == 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcount(\"schedule\", scheduled)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn report, err\n\t}\n\treport.Quiescence = s.quiescenceReason(state)\n\treport.NextHorizon = earlierHorizon(report.NextHorizon, s.nextHorizon(state))\n\tif err := s.persistFoldSnapshot(); err != nil {\n\t\t// D0009.C9 makes this projection destroyable: failure cannot weaken or\n\t\t// block a drive session, and the next process safely performs a cold fold.\n\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, \"fold snapshot not persisted: \"+err.Error())\n\t}\n\n\tsessionSeq, err := s.appendSessionRecord(state, cursorStart, report)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn report, err\n\t}\n\treport.SessionSeq = sessionSeq\n\tif err := s.writeProjections(state, report); err != nil {\n\t\treturn report, err\n\t}\n\treturn report, nil\n}\n\nfunc appendWakeProjectionNote(report *Report, err error) {\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tnote := \"liveness floor unarmed: \" + err.Error()\n\tfor _, existing := range report.Notes {\n\t\tif existing == note {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, note)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) fold() (*State, error) {\n\tif s.foldState == nil {\n\t\tif snapshot, scan, snapshotAdmissionClean, snapshotAuthorityClean, snapshotStalenessClean, *Session) recoverAuthorityEffects() (int, error) {\n\tif s.authorityRecoveryClean {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\tactions, err := recoverAuthorityEffects(s.Graph, s.Now)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\ts.authorityRecoveryClean = true\n\t}\n\treturn actions, err\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) expandAnchoredProduct(product changeset.Product) (changeset.Product, error) {\n\tif product.Anchor == nil
