*Session) undrainedSubmissions() ([]pendingSubmission, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tsubmissionsDir := filepath.Join(s.Spool.Root, \"spool\", \"submissions\")\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(submissionsDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tvar pending []pendingSubmission\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif !entry.IsDir() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tdir := filepath.Join(submissionsDir, entry.Name())\n\t\tif state.Drained[entry.Name()] || state.ImportedSubmissions[entry.Name()] {\n\t\t\t// V1 drained records and v2 durable imports both make the spool copy\n\t\t\t// disposable. In v2, recovery continues from object pins even though\n\t\t\t// intake itself does not claim dispatch_effects_complete.\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tmanifest, loadErr := backend.ReadSubmissionManifest(filepath.Join(dir, \"manifest.json\"))\n\t\tpending = append(pending, pendingSubmission{dispatchID: entry.Name(), dir: dir, manifest: manifest, loadErr: loadErr})\n\t}\n\t// Canonical drain order: dispatch_id, then manifest end time.\n\tsort.Slice(pending, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\tif pending[i].dispatchID != pending[j].dispatchID {\n\t\t\treturn pending[i].dispatchID < pending[j].dispatchID\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn pending[i].manifest.Attribution.EndedAt < pending[j].manifest.Attribution.EndedAt\n\t})\n\treturn pending, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) drain(report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tactions, err := s.recoverDurableAdmissions(report)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tpending, err := s.undrainedSubmissions()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t}\n\tfor _, item := range pending {\n\t\tif item.loadErr != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: committed submission %s cannot recover exact context: %w: %v\", item.dispatchID, ErrDispatchRecoveryParked, item.loadErr)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif item.manifest.SchemaVersion != 1 {\n\t\t\ts.admissionRecoveryClean = false\n\t\t\tresult, err := s.Graph.AdmitCommittedSubmissionV2(item.dir, item.dispatchID, store.AdmitV2Options{\n\t\t\t\tAdmitOptions: store.AdmitOptions{Kinds: s.Kinds, Now: s.Now, Instance: s.Instance},\n\t\t\t\tSealKey:      s.SealKeys[item.manifest.Attribution.BackendID],\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, store.ErrCommittedSubmissionRecoveryParked) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: committed submission %s: %w: %v\", item.dispatchID, ErrDispatchRecoveryParked, err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif result.DispatchEffectsComplete {\n\t\t\t\treport.Drained = appendOnce(report.Drained, item.dispatchID)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := item.manifest.VerifySealWith(s.SealKeys[item.manifest.Attribution.BackendID]); err != nil {\n\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"submission_refused\", \"admission\", nil, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"dispatch_id\": item.dispatchID,\n\t\t\t\t\"run_ref\":     int64(item.manifest.RunRef),\n\t\t\t\t\"lane_id\":     item.manifest.LaneID,\n\t\t\t\t\"attempt\":     item.manifest.Attempt,\n\t\t\t\t\"reason\":      string(store.RefusalSealInvalid),\n\t\t\t\t\"detail\":      err.Error(),\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treport.Drained = append(report.Drained, item.dispatchID)\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tsub, err := s.submissionFromBundle(state, item)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult, err := s.Graph.Admit(sub, store.AdmitOptions{Kinds: s.Kinds, Now: s.Now, Instance: s.Instance})\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.BundleRefusal == \"\" && !result.SubmittedLate {\n\t\t\tswitch item.manifest.Status {\n\t\t\tcase \"complete\", \"\":\n\t\t\t\tpayload := map[string]any{\"run_ref\": int64(sub.RunRef), \"outcome\": \"submitted\"}\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"pass_run_closed\", \"driver\", []uint64{sub.RunRef}, payload); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// Integration losers surface as typed conflict records (\u00a77.3).\n\t\t\t\tif n, err := s.surfaceIntegrationConflicts(sub.RunRef, item.dir); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tactions += n\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase \"partial\", \"failed\":\n\t\t\t\t// Failed and partial integration submissions may still carry typed\n\t\t\t\t// conflict exhaust. Surface it once, even when no terminal report is\n\t\t\t\t// available to close the run before the horizon.\n\t\t\t\tif n, err := s.surfaceIntegrationConflicts(sub.RunRef, item.dir); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tactions += n\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\trun := state.Runs[sub.RunRef]\n\t\t\t\tterminal, err := s.loadTerminalReport(item.dispatchID)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif closed, err := s.closeRunFromTerminalReport(state, run, terminal, s.Now().UTC(), report); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t} else if closed {\n\t\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t\t} else if closed, err := s.closeRunFromFailedSubmission(run, item.manifest); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t} else if closed {\n\t\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treport.Drained = append(report.Drained, item.dispatchID)\n\t\tactions++\n\t}\n\tif n, err := s.recoverDurableAdmissions(report); err != nil {\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t} else {\n\t\tactions += n\n\t}\n\trecoveredActions, err := s.closePreviouslyDrainedFailedSubmissions()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t}\n\tactions += recoveredActions\n\treportActions, err := s.drainTerminalReports(report)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t}\n\treturn actions + reportActions, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) closePreviouslyDrainedFailedSubmissions() (int, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tdispatches := make([]string, 0, len(state.Submissions))\n\tfor dispatchID := range state.Submissions {\n\t\tdispatches = append(dispatches, dispatchID)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(dispatches)\n\n\tactions := 0\n\tfor _, dispatchID := range dispatches {\n\t\tstate, err = s.fold()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsub := state.Submissions[dispatchID]\n\t\tif sub.Status != \"failed\" && sub.Status != \"partial\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\trun := state.Runs[sub.RunRef]\n\t\tif run == nil || !run.Open() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tmanifest := s.manifestForDrainedSubmission(sub)\n\t\tif closed, err := s.closeRunFromFailedSubmission(run, manifest); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t} else if closed {\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) manifestForDrainedSubmission(sub SubmissionState) backend.SubmissionManifest {\n\tminimal := backend.SubmissionManifest{DispatchID: sub.DispatchID, RunRef: sub.RunRef, Status: sub.Status}\n\tmanifest, err := backend.ReadSubmissionManifest(filepath.Join(s.Spool.SubmissionDir(sub.DispatchID), \"manifest.json\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn minimal\n\t}\n\tif manifest.DispatchID != sub.DispatchID || manifest.RunRef != sub.RunRef || defaultString(manifest.Status, \"complete\") != sub.Status {\n\t\treturn minimal\n\t}\n\tif err := manifest.VerifySealWith(s.SealKeys[manifest.Attribution.BackendID]); err != nil {\n\t\treturn minimal\n\t}\n\treturn manifest\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) closeRunFromFailedSubmission(run *RunState, manifest backend.SubmissionManifest) (bool, error) {\n\tif run == nil || !run.Open() {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tstatus := manifest.Status\n\tif status == \"\" {\n\t\tstatus = \"complete\"\n\t}\n\tstage := run.StepID\n\tif stage == \"\" {\n\t\tstage = run.PassID\n\t}\n\tif stage == \"\" {\n\t\tstage = \"submission_drain\"\n\t}\n\tmissing := missingOutputs(manifest)\n\tdetail := fmt.Sprintf(\"submission %s drained with status %q\", manifest.DispatchID, status)\n\tif len(missing) > 0 {\n\t\tdetail = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s; missing required outputs: %v\", detail, missing)\n\t}\n\tif len(manifest.Outputs) == 0 {\n\t\tdetail += \"; no outputs were submitted\"\n\t}\n\tpayload := s.failureClosure(run.Seq, stage, \"runtime_exit\", detail, []uint64{run.Seq})\n\tpayload[\"closure_source\"] = \"submission_status\"\n\tpayload[\"dispatch_id\"] = manifest.DispatchID\n\tpayload[\"submission_status\"] = status\n\tpayload[\"missing_outputs\"] = missing\n\tif _, err := s.append(\"pass_run_closed\", \"driver\", []uint64{run.Seq}, payload); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\nfunc missingOutputs(manifest backend.SubmissionManifest) []any {\n\tvar missing []any\n\tfor _, output := range manifest.Outputs {\n\t\tif output.Status == \"missing\" {\n\t\t\tmissing = append(missing, output.OutputID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif missing == nil {\n\t\tmissing = []any{}\n\t}\n\treturn missing\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) drainTerminalReports(report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tactions := 0\n\tfor _, runSeq := range sortedRunSeqs(state) {\n\t\tstate, err = s.fold()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\trun := state.Runs[runSeq]\n\t\tif run == nil || !run.Open() || run.DispatchID == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tterminal, err := s.loadTerminalReport(run.DispatchID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tclosed, err := s.closeRunFromTerminalReport(state, run, terminal, s.Now().UTC(), report)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif closed {\n\t\t\treport.Drained = append(report.Drained, run.DispatchID)\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) terminalReportPath(dispatchID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(s.Spool.Root, \"spool\", \"reports\", dispatchID, \"terminal_report.json\")\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) retainedSupervisorLogPath(dispatchID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(s.Spool.Root, \"spool\", \"retained-logs\", dispatchID, \"supervisor.log\")\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) loadTerminalReport(dispatchID string) (*spoolclose.Report, error) {\n\tif dispatchID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\traw, err := os.ReadFile(s.terminalReportPath(dispatchID))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tvar report spoolclose.Report\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &report); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tswitch report.ExitReason {\n\tcase spoolclose.BudgetExpiry, spoolclose.RelaunchExhausted, spoolclose.RuntimeExit:\n\t\treturn &report, nil\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) closeRunFromTerminalReport(state *State, run *RunState, terminal *spoolclose.Report, now time.Time, report *Report) (bool, error) {\n\tif run == nil || !run.Open() || terminal == nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tdecision := spoolclose.ScanClose(s.laneForReport(state, run, terminal, now))\n\tif !decision.Close {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tswitch decision.Mode {\n\tcase spoolclose.ModeProvisionalAbandon, spoolclose.ModeReportedTerminal:\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tif _, err := s.closeFromScan(run, decision, terminal, now); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\ts.courtesyAbort(run, report)\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) laneForReport(state *State, run *RunState, terminal *spoolclose.Report, now time.Time) spoolclose.Lane {\n\treturn spoolclose.Lane{\n\t\tLaneID:         run.LaneID,\n\t\tAgeSeconds:     durationSecondsInt(observedActive(run, now)),\n\t\tHorizonSeconds: durationSecondsInt(s.Policy.DeadlineFor(run.DeadlineClass)),\n\t\tIsWinner:       state.LaneWon(run),\n\t\tReport:         terminal,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) closeFromScan(run *RunState, decision spoolclose.CloseDecision, terminal *spoolclose.Report, now time.Time) (store.AppendResult, error) {\n\tpayload := map[string]any{\n\t\t\"run_ref\":             int64(run.Seq),\n\t\t\"closure_source\":      \"terminal_report\",\n\t\t\"scan_close_mode\":     string(decision.Mode),\n\t\t\"exit_reason\":         string(terminal.ExitReason),\n\t\t\"supervisor_tail\":     terminal.SupervisorTail,\n\t\t\"supervisor_log_path\": s.retainedSupervisorLogPath(run.DispatchID),\n\t\t\"closure_reason\":      decision.Reason,\n\t\t\"observed_active\":     int64(observedActive(run, now) / time.Second),\n\t}\n\tswitch decision.Mode {\n\tcase spoolclose.ModeProvisionalAbandon:\n\t\tpayload[\"outcome\"] = \"abandoned\"\n\t\tpayload[\"cause\"] = \"exit_reported\"\n\tcase spoolclose.ModeReportedTerminal:\n\t\tpayload[\"outcome\"] = \"error\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn store.AppendResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: terminal report scan returned unsupported close mode %q\", decision.Mode)\n\t}\n\tif failure := s.failureValue(string(terminal.ExitReason), string(terminal.ExitReason), decision.Reason, []uint64{run.Seq}); failure != nil {\n\t\tpayload[\"failure\"] = failure\n\t}\n\treturn s.appendPassRunClosedV2([]uint64{run.Seq}, payload)\n}\n\nfunc observedActive(run *RunState, now time.Time) time.Duration {\n\tif run == nil || run.OpenedAt.IsZero() || now.Before(run.OpenedAt) {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn now.Sub(run.OpenedAt)\n}\n\nfunc durationSecondsInt(duration time.Duration) int {\n\tif duration <= 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tseconds := duration / time.Second\n\tif seconds > time.Duration(^uint(0)>>1) {\n\t\treturn int(^uint(0) >> 1)\n\t}\n\treturn int(seconds)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) submissionFromBundle(state *State, item pendingSubmission) (store.Submission, error) {\n\trun := state.Runs[item.manifest.RunRef]\n\tvar inputPins []store.Pin\n\tvar subjectPin *store.Pin\n\tif run != nil {\n\t\tif pins, ok := run.Manifest[\"input_pins\"].([]any); ok {\n\t\t\tfor _, entry := range pins {\n\t\t\t\tpin, _ := entry.(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tfoldedPin := store.Pin{\n\t\t\t\t\tRepoID: str(pin, \"repo_id\"), Identity: str(pin, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\t\t\tVersionSeq: num(pin, \"version_seq\"), ContentHash: str(pin, \"content_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\t\tAnchor: str(pin, \"anchor\"), ElementHash: str(pin, \"element_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tinputPins = append(inputPins, foldedPin)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif pin, ok := run.Manifest[\"subject_pin\"].(map[string]any); ok {\n\t\t\tsubjectPin = &store.Pin{\n\t\t\t\tIdentity:    str(pin, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\t\tVersionSeq:  num(pin, \"version_seq\"),\n\t\t\t\tContentHash: str(pin, \"content_hash\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tsub := store.Submission{\n\t\tDispatchID:       item.manifest.DispatchID,\n\t\tRunRef:           item.manifest.RunRef,\n\t\tRunManifestHash:  item.manifest.RunManifestHash,\n\t\tLaneID:           item.manifest.LaneID,\n\t\tAttempt:          item.manifest.Attempt,\n\t\tStatus:           item.manifest.Status,\n\t\tEnvironmentReads: item.manifest.EnvironmentReads,\n\t\tAttribution: store.Attribution{\n\t\t\tBackendID:      item.manifest.Attribution.BackendID,\n\t\t\tBackendVersion: item.manifest.Attribution.BackendVersion,\n\t\t\tAliasingClass:  item.manifest.Attribution.AliasingClass,\n\t\t\tAgentRuntime:   item.manifest.Attribution.AgentRuntimeID,\n\t\t\tLaneID:         item.manifest.LaneID,\n\t\t\tLaneRole:       item.manifest.Attribution.LaneRole,\n\t\t\tSessionNonce:   item.manifest.Attribution.SessionNonce,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\tfor _, entry := range item.manifest.Outputs {\n\t\tif entry.Status != \"present\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tbody, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(item.dir, entry.Path))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn sub, err\n\t\t}\n\t\toutput := store.SubmissionOutput{\n\t\t\tOutputID:           entry.OutputID,\n\t\t\tKind:               entry.Kind,\n\t\t\tIdentity:           entry.Identity,\n\t\t\tPlacement:          entry.Placement,\n\t\t\tBody:               body,\n\t\t\tContentHash:        entry.ContentHash,\n\t\t\tDerivedFromOutputs: entry.DerivedFrom,\n\t\t}\n\t\trole := \"\"\n\t\tif kind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[entry.Kind]; ok {\n\t\t\trole = kind.Role\n\t\t}\n\t\tif role == \"evidence\" && subjectPin != nil {\n\t\t\t// Mirror the evidence verdict into the evidences edge claim so\n\t\t\t// gate and re-stamp folds never open evidence bodies.\n\t\t\toutput.Edges = append(output.Edges, store.Edge{\n\t\t\t\tKind:           \"evidences\",\n\t\t\t\tPin:            *subjectPin,\n\t\t\t\tClaim:          evidenceClaim(entry.Kind, body),\n\t\t\t\tBindingClosure: []store.Pin{*subjectPin},\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t// A v2 Verification Report may aggregate Receipts admitted by an\n\t\t\t// earlier root/residual run. Preserve those reused inputs as exact\n\t\t\t// admitted Receipt-version provenance in addition to the report's\n\t\t\t// ordinary evidences edge; same-bundle Receipt output ids are\n\t\t\t// resolved by the store admission boundary.\n\t\t\tif entry.Kind == \"verification-report\" && run != nil {\n\t\t\t\tverification, _ := run.Manifest[\"verification\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tif intOf(verification, \"schema_version\") == 2 {\n\t\t\t\t\tfor _, pin := range inputPins {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tidentity := state.Identities[pin.Identity]\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif identity == nil || pin.VersionSeq == 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t\tversion := identity.Versions[pin.VersionSeq]\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif version == nil || version.Kind != \"receipt\" || version.ContentHash != pin.ContentHash {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t\toutput.Edges = append(output.Edges, store.Edge{Kind: \"derived_from\", Pin: pin})\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// Provenance edges are the sealed manifest's input pins. A pin with\n\t\t\t// no identity is content-addressed context (e.g. a build's composed\n\t\t\t// base \u2014 the tree it edits, D0010.C5), never a provenance parent.\n\t\t\tfor _, pin := range inputPins {\n\t\t\t\tif pin.Identity == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\toutput.Edges = append(output.Edges, store.Edge{Kind: \"derived_from\", Pin: pin})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tsub.Outputs = append(sub.Outputs, output)\n\t}\n\treturn sub, nil\n}\n\n// --- progress: gate evaluation and head movement ---\n\nfunc (s *Session) progress(report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tif recovered, err := s.recoverPreparedIntegrationEffects(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t} else if recovered > 0 {\n\t\treturn recovered, nil\n\t}\n\tif err := s.verifyIntegrationEffectPairs(state); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tif recovered, err := s.recoverIntegrationApplications(state); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t} else if recovered > 0 {\n\t\treturn recovered, nil\n\t}\n\t// A failed integration-checks CAS and its typed tree_moved diagnostic are\n\t// one resumable Driver transition. Close that ledger-only cut before any\n\t// planner can restage or rebuild from the failure.\n\tif recovered, err := s.recoverTreeMovedDiagnostics(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t} else if recovered > 0 {\n\t\treturn recovered, nil\n\t}\n\tactions := 0\n\tlegacyEra := 0\n\tfor _, id := range sortedIdentityIDs(state) {\n\t\tidentity := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tif n, err := s.restampStaleHead(state, identity); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t} else if n > 0 {\n\t\t\tactions += n\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcandidate := latestCandidate(identity)\n\t\tif candidate == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif legacyEraCandidate(state, candidate) {\n\t\t\t// Era bound (fold-compat): a candidate produced under a request\n\t\t\t// with no exact target projection keeps its recorded gate\n\t\t\t// history. Attempting a fresh v2 stamp would demand an exact\n\t\t\t// context its era never recorded; new exact demands plan new\n\t\t\t// work instead.\n\t\t\tlegacyEra++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[candidate.Kind]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind.Role == \"evidence\" {\n\t\t\t// Evidence never head-moves, but its check-class gates run:\n\t\t\t// receipt-checks and verification-report-checks validate the\n\t\t\t// candidate so claims can count it (RFC 0010 \u00a75.5). Every\n\t\t\t// version still lacking an applicable result is stamped, not\n\t\t\t// only the latest: a superseded-but-qualifying report that\n\t\t\t// never earns its gate is invisible to the Dual-Signal Rule,\n\t\t\t// and that gap re-minted both signals every session until the\n\t\t\t// planner livelocked (2026-07-12; progressEvidence itself\n\t\t\t// skips versions already applicably stamped, so this loop is\n\t\t\t// cheap once stamped).\n\t\t\tfor _, seq := range identity.Order {\n\t\t\t\tversion := identity.Versions[seq]\n\t\t\t\tif version == nil || legacyEraCandidate(state, version) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tn, err := s.progressEvidence(state, identity, version, kind)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tactions += n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tn, err := s.progressCandidate(state, identity, candidate, kind, report)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tactions += n\n\t}\n\tif legacyEra > 0 {\n\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, fmt.Sprintf(\"legacy-era candidates keep their recorded gate history: %d left unstamped\", legacyEra))\n\t}\n\t// progressCandidate may have just durably written the failed E2 gate. Close\n\t// that new prefix in this same progress phase so the following plan phase\n\t// necessarily sees the exact diagnostic rather than inferring one from\n\t// hashes. The opening recovery above handles process interruption here.\n\tif n, err := s.recoverTreeMovedDiagnostics(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t} else {\n\t\tactions += n\n\t}\n\t// The successful-CAS twin of the diagnostic closure above: progressCandidate\n\t// may have just written an integration head movement, and until every one of\n\t// its applications is projected the applied integration is invisible to\n\t// applicationBound \u2014 the following plan phase re-stages the already-applied\n\t// step and the duplicate's no-op movement advances the Product version over\n\t// evidence bound to the previous head (observed live 2026-07-15: plan 64137\n\t// re-opened integration as run 64138 fourteen seconds after movement 64135;\n\t// duplicate movement 64705 voided both freshly passed report gates). Drained\n\t// to zero, not recovered once: one movement can carry a batch of applications.\n\tfor {\n\t\tdrained, err := s.fold()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tn, err := s.recoverIntegrationApplications(drained)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif n == 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tactions += n\n\t}\n\tif n, err := s.surfaceFlakes(state); err != nil {\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t} else {\n\t\tactions += n\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\n// progressEvidence evaluates check-class gates on an evidence candidate.\n// Evidence has no head to move; the gate results are what claim computation\n// and the Dual-Signal Rule read.\nfunc (s *Session) progressEvidence(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, kind KindSpec) (int, error) {\n\tactions := 0\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn actions, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: gate %q not cataloged\", gateID)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif spec.Class != \"check\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue // evidence faces mechanical validation only\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tif latest := latestResult(state.GateResults[key]); latest != nil && s.gateResultApplicable(state, identity, candidate, spec, *latest) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif gateID == \"verification-report-checks\" && !s.reportReceiptGatesReady(state, candidate) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\toutcome, detail := s.evaluateCheckGate(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\tif _, err := s.appendGateResult(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, \"\", detail, true); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tactions++\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) reportReceiptGatesReady(state *State, candidate *VersionState) bool {\n\tif candidate == nil || candidate.Kind != \"verification-report\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tfor _, pin := range candidate.DerivedFrom {\n\t\tidentity := state.Identities[pin.Identity]\n\t\tif identity == nil || pin.VersionSeq == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tversion := identity.Versions[pin.VersionSeq]\n\t\tif version == nil || version.Kind != \"receipt\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tspec, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[\"receipt-checks\"]\n\t\tlatest := latestResult(state.GateResults[gateKey{Identity: pin.Identity, Version: pin.VersionSeq, GateID: \"receipt-checks\"}])\n\t\tif !ok || latest == nil || latest.Outcome != \"pass\" || !s.gateResultApplicable(state, identity, version, spec, *latest) {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// restampEdge classifies one edge of a head for the licensed re-stamp: moves\n// reports whether the edge is freshness-bearing (derived_from/constrained_by,\n// non-diagnostic) and therefore repoints at its identity's current head.\ntype restampEdge struct {\n\tedge  store.Edge\n\tmoves bool\n\t// diagnostic is true only for an exact role=diagnostic producer-manifest\n\t// pin, whose projection may be removed. Intrinsic non-freshness edges\n\t// (self/evidence/refused) remain carried unchanged.\n\tdiagnostic bool\n}\n\nfunc artifactVersionEdges(version *VersionState) []store.Edge {\n\tif version == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tedges := append([]store.Edge(nil), version.Edges...)\n\tif len(edges) == 0 {\n\t\tfor _, pin := range version.DerivedFrom {\n\t\t\tedges = append(edges, store.Edge{Kind: \"derived_from\", Pin: pin})\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, pin := range version.ConstrainedBy {\n\t\t\tedges = append(edges, store.Edge{Kind: \"constrained_by\", Pin: pin})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn edges\n}\n\n// restampEdges returns the effective edge set a re-stamp of head walks \u2014 the\n// head's recorded edges, or the derived_from/constrained_by fallback for\n// pre-edge bodies \u2014 with each edge classified once, so the progress sweep and\n// the demand planner share one definition of \"freshness-bearing prerequisite\".\nfunc restampEdges(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState) []restampEdge {\n\tdiagnostics := state.diagnosticPinsForVersion(identityID, head)\n\tedges := artifactVersionEdges(head)\n\tclassified := make([]restampEdge, 0, len(edges))\n\tfor _, edge := range edges {\n\t\tfreshnessKind := edge.Kind == \"derived_from\" || edge.Kind == \"constrained_by\"\n\t\texplicitDiagnostic := edge.Kind == \"derived_from\" && diagnostics[edge.Pin] > 0\n\t\tif explicitDiagnostic {\n\t\t\tdiagnostics[edge.Pin]--\n\t\t}\n\t\tlifecycleDiagnostic := freshnessKind && (explicitDiagnostic || state.intrinsicDiagnosticArtifactPin(identityID, edge.Pin))\n\t\tmoves := freshnessKind && !lifecycleDiagnostic\n\t\tclassified = append(classified, restampEdge{edge: edge, moves: moves, diagnostic: explicitDiagnostic})\n\t}\n\treturn classified\n}\n\n// restampReplacementEdges computes the exact replacement vector that must be\n// sealed before a revalidation review runs. It never selects a stale or\n// missing upstream. Diagnostic pins stay in the immutable producer manifest\n// and predecessor artifact, but do not acquire lifecycle force in the new\n// artifact occurrence.\nfunc restampReplacementEdges(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState) ([]store.Edge, bool) {\n\tclassified := restampEdges(state, identityID, head)\n\treplacements := make([]store.Edge, 0, len(classified))\n\tfor _, entry := range classified {\n\t\tedge := entry.edge\n\t\tif entry.diagnostic {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif entry.moves {\n\t\t\tif edge.Pin.RepoID != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t// Foreign freshness resolves through locally admitted head\n\t\t\t\t// attestations, not a same-id local artifact head. Until the\n\t\t\t\t// replacement vector can encode that proof, fail closed.\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tupstream := state.HeadVersion(edge.Pin.Identity)\n\t\t\tif upstream == nil || upstream.Stale {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treplacement := store.Pin{Identity: edge.Pin.Identity, VersionSeq: upstream.Seq, ContentHash: upstream.ContentHash}\n\t\t\tif edge.Pin.Anchor != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\telementHash := upstream.Anchors[edge.Pin.Anchor]\n\t\t\t\tif elementHash == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, false\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treplacement.Anchor = edge.Pin.Anchor\n\t\t\t\treplacement.ElementHash = elementHash\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tedge.Pin = replacement\n\t\t}\n\t\treplacements = append(replacements, edge)\n\t}\n\treturn replacements, true\n}\n\n// revalidationContextEdges returns the edge vector sealed into a revalidation\n// manifest. An accepting outcome can license a re-stamp only when the exact\n// replacement vector resolves. A Tier-0 mechanical refusal needs no such\n// license: when the replacement topology itself is no longer representable\n// (for example, a packet anchor disappeared from the current Work Graph), the\n// unchanged predecessor vector is the exact non-moving context for the\n// refusing Review Ledger. The failed Check result keeps that context\n// non-licensing in both the Driver and Store, while allowing the evidence to\n// admit and route producer regeneration instead of silently dropping demand.\nfunc revalidationContextEdges(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState, evaluation revalidationGateEvaluation) ([]store.Edge, bool) {\n\treplacements, ready := restampReplacementEdges(state, identityID, head)\n\tif ready {\n\t\treturn replacements, true\n\t}\n\tif evaluation.EvaluationTier == 0 && evaluation.Verdict == \"needs_revision\" {\n\t\treturn artifactVersionEdges(head), true\n\t}\n\treturn nil, false\n}\n\n// restampBlockedUpstreams returns the freshness-bearing prerequisite pins\n// whose current heads block the licensed re-stamp of head: identities whose\n// accepted head is missing or stale.\nfunc restampBlockedUpstreams(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState) []store.Pin {\n\tvar blocked []store.Pin\n\tfor _, classified := range restampEdges(state, identityID, head) {\n\t\tif !classified.moves {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tupstream := state.HeadVersion(classified.edge.Pin.Identity)\n\t\tif upstream == nil || upstream.Stale {\n\t\t\tblocked = append(blocked, classified.edge.Pin)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn blocked\n}\n\nfunc revalidationStepID(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState) string {\n\tbase := \"revalidate/\" + identityID\n\tif head == nil {\n\t\treturn base\n\t}\n\tprior := candidateBoundReviewRevisionsSinceLicense(state, identityID, head.Seq, head.ContentHash)\n\tif prior == 0 {\n\t\treturn base\n\t}\n\t// Review artifacts are immutable. If an older outcome is inapplicable\n\t// under the current sealed context, a salted objective is required; the\n\t// winner rule must not make the repaired exact-context review impossible.\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%d-r%d\", base, head.Seq, prior)\n}\n\nfunc revalidationPlanStep(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState, reason string) planStep {\n\treturn planStep{\n\t\tStepID:     revalidationStepID(state, identityID, head),\n\t\tPassID:     \"review\",\n\t\tSubject:    identityID,\n\t\tRevalidate: true,\n\t\tReason:     reason,\n\t}\n}\n\n// planInvalidatedRevalidation consumes a closed non-accepting revalidation\n// outcome by rerunning the artifact's own producer with that Review Ledger as\n// a diagnostic. This is the invalidated branch of D0005.C4: the Driver plans;\n// the producing pass creates a new version. It never converts a failed Check\n// into a re-stamp or asks a reviewer to override the mechanical result.\nfunc (s *Session) planInvalidatedRevalidation(state *State, request *RequestState, identityID string, head *VersionState, steps *[]planStep) bool {\n\tledgerID, outcome := s.findRevalidationOutcome(state, identityID, head)\n\tif outcome == nil || gate.ReviewVerdictAccepting(outcome.ReviewVerdict) {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tidentityState := state.Identities[identityID]\n\tif identityState == nil {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tstep, ok := s.exactContextRegenerationStep(state, request, head.Kind, identityID, identityState, head,\n\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"revalidation %s: regenerate from the exact stale version and diagnostic delta\", outcome.ReviewVerdict))\n\tif !ok {\n\t\t// An applicable closed outcome owns this frontier. Falling through would\n\t\t// redispatch the already-closed review and can only burn retry budget.\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\trevalidationRun := state.Runs[outcome.ProducedByRun]\n\tif revalidationRun == nil || revalidationRun.ManifestHash == \"\" || revalidationRun.RequestRef == 0 {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tstep.DiagnosticIDs = appendUniqueDiagnostic(step.DiagnosticIDs, ledgerID)\n\tstep.RecoveryContext = map[string]any{\n\t\t\"kind\": \"revalidation_invalidated\",\n\t\t\"subject\": driverPinValue(store.Pin{\n\t\t\tIdentity: identityID, VersionSeq: head.Seq, ContentHash: head.ContentHash,\n\t\t}),\n\t\t\"review_ledger\": driverPinValue(store.Pin{\n\t\t\tIdentity: ledgerID, VersionSeq: outcome.Seq, ContentHash: outcome.ContentHash,\n\t\t}),\n\t\t\"revalidation_run\": map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"run_ref\":             int64(revalidationRun.Seq),\n\t\t\t\"run_manifest_hash\":   revalidationRun.ManifestHash,\n\t\t\t\"request_ref\":         int64(revalidationRun.RequestRef),\n\t\t\t\"target_context_hash\": str(revalidationRun.Manifest, \"target_context_hash\"),\n\t\t},\n\t\t\"verdict\": outcome.ReviewVerdict,\n\t}\n\t*steps = append(*steps, step)\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// planMechanicallyInvalidatedUnrevalidatable routes the narrow case where\n// current Check semantics already invalidate a stale head but an exact\n// revalidation run cannot be sealed because the original producer contract or\n// semantic environment moved. Revalidation has been attempted and proved\n// inapplicable; redispatching a review can never create a valid C10 origin\n// preimage. The lawful demand is therefore a fresh run of the producing pass\n// under the current contract, carrying the invalidated occurrence as its\n// diagnostic. Judgment failures still require a Review Ledger and never enter\n// this branch.\nfunc (s *Session) planMechanicallyInvalidatedUnrevalidatable(state *State, request *RequestState, identityID string, head *VersionState, steps *[]planStep) bool {\n\tif head == nil || !head.Stale {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[head.Kind]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tevaluation, classified := s.evaluateRevalidationGates(state, identityID, head, kind)\n\tif !classified || evaluation.EvaluationTier != 0 || evaluation.Verdict != \"needs_revision\" {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif _, proven := s.restampOriginProducer(state, identityID, head); proven {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tidentityState := state.Identities[identityID]\n\tif identityState == nil {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tdetails := make([]string, 0, len(evaluation.Mechanical))\n\tfor _, result := range evaluation.Mechanical {\n\t\tif result.Outcome == \"fail\" {\n\t\t\tdetails = append(details, result.GateID+\": \"+result.Detail)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tstep, ready := s.exactContextRegenerationStep(state, request, head.Kind, identityID, identityState, head,\n\t\t\"mechanical revalidation invalidated the stale occurrence, but its origin producer contract or semantic environment moved; regenerate under the current producer: \"+strings.Join(details, \"; \"))\n\tif !ready {\n\t\t// An older root Change Set can predate the explicit authority-bridge\n\t\t// marker while still carrying the exact origin Work Graph pin needed to\n\t\t// prove continuity. If the current accepted Work Graph contains exactly\n\t\t// one lawful replacement authority packet, issue the same marked no-op\n\t\t// subject build as Product-side recovery. The packet declaration supplies\n\t\t// execution authority; the evaluator declaration alone never does.\n\t\tif bridge, bridgeReady := s.invalidatedRootTargetCheckAuthorityBridgeStep(state, request, identityID, head); bridgeReady {\n\t\t\t*steps = append(*steps, bridge)\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t\t// A retired target-Check authority bridge has one additional lawful\n\t\t// upstream recovery. If the accepted current Work Graph contains no\n\t\t// exact packet carrying the evaluator Checks, revising the fresh\n\t\t// accepted L3 is required before a replacement build can exist. The\n\t\t// target declaration itself grants no execution authority (D0010.C12).\n\t\tif targetCheckAuthorityBridgeVersion(state, head) && request != nil && request.TargetProjection != nil && s.Checks != nil {\n\t\t\tplanID := subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"implementation-plan\")\n\t\t\tplanState := state.Identities[planID]\n\t\t\tplanHead := state.HeadVersion(planID)\n\t\t\trequired := s.Checks.Registry.Set(request.TargetProjection.Evaluation.CheckSetID)\n\t\t\tif planState != nil && planHead != nil && len(required) > 0 &&\n\t\t\t\ts.targetCheckAuthorityPlanRevisionAvailable(state, request, planID, planHead, head, required) {\n\t\t\t\t// This is a nested recovery frontier: the target conjunct names\n\t\t\t\t// the stale Change Set, so the ordinary candidate-first guard\n\t\t\t\t// never visits the upstream Implementation Plan identity. Once\n\t\t\t\t// the requested revision has produced a candidate, that exact\n\t\t\t\t// candidate owns the frontier through its ordinary gates. Opening\n\t\t\t\t// another salted implementation-planning run here would bypass\n\t\t\t\t// review and can repeat forever without any refusing evidence.\n\t\t\t\tif pending := latestCandidate(planState); pending != nil && pending.Seq > planHead.Seq &&\n\t\t\t\t\t!s.candidateRetired(state, planState, pending) {\n\t\t\t\t\tplanned := map[string]bool{planID: true}\n\t\t\t\t\ts.progressPendingCandidate(state, request, \"implementation-plan\", planID, planState, pending, planned, steps)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tplanStep, planReady := s.exactContextRegenerationStep(state, request, \"implementation-plan\", planID, planState, planHead,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"the retired target-Check authority bridge cannot be regenerated from the accepted current Work Graph; \"+targetCheckAuthorityRevisionReason(request, required))\n\t\t\t\tif planReady {\n\t\t\t\t\tplanStep.DiagnosticIDs = appendUniqueDiagnostic(planStep.DiagnosticIDs,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tidentityID, productIdentity(request.Subject), subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"work-graph\"))\n\t\t\t\t\t*steps = append(*steps, planStep)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\t// The conclusively invalidated occurrence owns this frontier even when\n\t\t// its exact producer context has no lawful current selection. Falling\n\t\t// through would emit the impossible review again.\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\t*steps = append(*steps, step)\n\treturn true\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) invalidatedRootTargetCheckAuthorityBridgeStep(state *State, request *RequestState, identityID string, head *VersionState) (planStep, bool) {\n\tif state == nil || request == nil || request.TargetProjection == nil || s.Checks == nil || head == nil ||\n\t\tidentityID != subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"change-set\") || head.Kind != \"change-set\" ||\n\t\tmechanicalEvaluationSubjectIdentity(request) != productIdentity(request.Subject) {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\trequired := s.Checks.Registry.Set(request.TargetProjection.Evaluation.CheckSetID)\n\tif len(required) == 0 {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tchangeBody, err := s.getObject(head.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tchange, err := s.parseChangeSet(changeBody)\n\tif err != nil || change.SchemaVersion != 2 || change.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity == \"\" ||\n\t\tchange.Packet.WorkGraphHash == \"\" || change.Packet.PacketID == \"\" || change.Packet.PacketElementHash == \"\" {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tworkGraphID := subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"work-graph\")\n\tworkGraphHead := state.HeadVersion(workGraphID)\n\tif workGraphHead == nil || workGraphHead.Stale {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tworkGraphBody, err := s.getObject(workGraphHead.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tcurrent, err := workgraph.Parse(workGraphBody)\n\tif err != nil || current.SchemaVersion != 2 {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\t// The old packet identifier can lawfully have been retired and later\n\t// re-authored with a different element. The Product-side rebinding proof\n\t// deliberately rejects that shape because it has no packet execution\n\t// evidence yet. This stale-root branch runs later: require the unique new\n\t// packet's exact current Change Set head and its dual-signal evidence. That\n\t// proves the selected packet occurrence without treating a later reuse of\n\t// the retired identifier as continuity for the old occurrence.\n\tpacketID, packetCount, resolved := s.targetCheckAuthorityBridgeCandidates(request, change, current, required)\n\tif !resolved || packetCount != 1 || packetID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tpacketIdentity := packetChangeSetIdentity(request.Subject, packetID)\n\tpacketHead := state.HeadVersion(packetIdentity)\n\tif packetHead == nil || packetHead.Stale || packetHead.Kind != \"change-set\" ||\n\t\t!requiredChecksAuthorized(s.packetAcceptanceCheckIDs(packetHead), required) {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tpacketBody, err := s.getObject(packetHead.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tpacketChange, err := s.parseChangeSet(packetBody)\n\tif err != nil || packetChange.SchemaVersion != 2 || packetChange.Packet.WorkGraphIdentity != workGraphID ||\n\t\tpacketChange.Packet.WorkGraphHash != workGraphHead.ContentHash || packetChange.Packet.PacketID != packetID {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tpacketElement, err := workgraph.PacketElementHash(current, packetID)\n\tif err != nil || packetChange.Packet.PacketElementHash != packetElement {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\tverified, signals, err := s.dualSignalSatisfied(state, packetIdentity, packetHead)\n\tif err != nil || !verified || !signalsCarryChecks(signals, required) {\n\t\treturn planStep{}, false\n\t}\n\treturn targetCheckAuthorityBridgeBuildStep(identityID, head, workGraphID, packetID,\n\t\tchange.Packet.WorkGraphHash), true\n}\n\n// planLicensedRestampPrerequisites owns the planning frontier of a stale head\n// whose accepting revalidation Review Ledger is already admitted: the\n// re-stamp is licensed, so re-dispatching the review is a doomed duplicate \u2014\n// two identical submitted attempts collapse into one deterministic failure\n// and exhaust bounds (RQ-57950). When the licensed re-stamp is blocked by\n// stale freshness-bearing prerequisites, planning advances those in\n// dependency order instead; when nothing blocks it, the progress sweep\n// performs the re-stamp with no new dispatch and planning has nothing to\n// add. It reports whether it owned the frontier; false falls back to\n// ordinary revalidation planning.\nfunc (s *Session) planLicensedRestampPrerequisites(state *State, request *RequestState, identityID string, head *VersionState, planned map[string]bool, steps *[]planStep) bool {\n\tif head == nil || !head.Stale {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif _, ledger := s.findRevalidationLedger(state, identityID, head); ledger == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t// A complete license owns the subject whether it is ready to fire or is\n\t// waiting on prerequisites. progress consumes the ready case; planning\n\t// advances only the blocked case.\n\ts.planRestampPrerequisiteFrontier(state, request, identityID, head, planned, map[string]bool{}, steps)\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// planRestampPrerequisites advances the deepest stale dependency before a\n// subject review is opened. This is required even when the subject has no\n// license yet: a C10 review must seal the exact replacement vector, which\n// cannot be known while any replacement head is itself stale.\nfunc (s *Session) planRestampPrerequisites(state *State, request *RequestState, identityID string, head *VersionState, planned map[string]bool, steps *[]planStep) bool {\n\treturn s.planRestampPrerequisiteFrontier(state, request, identityID, head, planned, map[string]bool{}, steps)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) planRestampPrerequisiteFrontier(state *State, request *RequestState, identityID string, head *VersionState, planned, visiting map[string]bool, steps *[]planStep) bool {\n\tif head == nil || !head.Stale || visiting[identityID] {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tblocked := restampBlockedUpstreams(state, identityID, head)\n\tif len(blocked) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tvisiting[identityID] = true\n\tdefer delete(visiting, identityID)\n\towned := false\n\tfor _, pin := range blocked {\n\t\tupstream := state.HeadVersion(pin.Identity)\n\t\tif upstream == nil {\n\t\t\t// A missing accepted head is production demand, not freshness\n\t\t\t// recovery; leave it to the conjunct machinery.\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif planned[pin.Identity] {\n\t\t\towned = true\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// A producer regeneration candidate above a stale prerequisite owns\n\t\t// that prerequisite's frontier \u2014 the same law the conjunct walk\n\t\t// applies to its own subject. Revalidating the superseded head while\n\t\t// its replacement is already in revision is a doomed dead end that\n\t\t// starves the candidate (live 2026-07-20: the split work-graph\n\t\t// candidate sat behind revalidate/<work-graph>@63949 for six hours\n\t\t// and the packetization rerun the amended continuity law licenses\n\t\t// was never planned).\n\t\tif upstreamState, ok := state.Identities[pin.Identity]; ok {\n\t\t\tif pending := latestCandidate(upstreamState); pending != nil && pending.Seq > upstream.Seq &&\n\t\t\t\t!s.candidateRetired(state, upstreamState, pending) {\n\t\t\t\tplanned[pin.Identity] = true\n\t\t\t\ts.progressPendingCandidate(state, request, pending.Kind, pin.Identity, upstreamState, pending, planned, steps)\n\t\t\t\towned = true\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.planRestampPrerequisiteFrontier(state, request, pin.Identity, upstream, planned, visiting, steps) {\n\t\t\tplanned[pin.Identity] = true\n\t\t\towned = true\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !upstream.Stale {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tplanned[pin.Identity] = true\n\t\tif _, ledger := s.findRevalidationLedger(state, pin.Identity, upstream); ledger != nil {\n\t\t\t// progress will consume this ready license on the next iteration;\n\t\t\t// another review would borrow or duplicate context.\n\t\t\towned = true\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.planInvalidatedRevalidation(state, request, pin.Identity, upstream, steps) {\n\t\t\towned = true\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Revalidation before regeneration (D0005.C3), one edge upstream:\n\t\t// the prerequisite either recovers through its own review or regenerates\n\t\t// from the admitted non-accepting outcome above.\n\t\t*steps = append(*steps, revalidationPlanStep(state, pin.Identity, upstream,\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"stale freshness prerequisite blocks exact revalidation of %s\", identityID)))\n\t\towned = true\n\t}\n\treturn owned\n}\n\n// restampStaleHead performs the licensed re-stamp of a stale accepted head\n// once an accepting revalidation Review Ledger has been admitted: the single\n// gate-exempt head movement (W6), attributed to the revalidation run.\nfunc (s *Session) restampStaleHead(state *State, identity *IdentityState) (int, error) {\n\tif identity.Head == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\thead := identity.Versions[identity.Head]\n\tif head == nil || !head.Stale {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\tledgerIdentity, ledgerVersion := s.findRevalidationLedger(state, identity.ID, head)\n\tif ledgerVersion == nil {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\trevalidation := state.Runs[ledgerVersion.ProducedByRun]\n\tcontext, err := store.ReStampRevalidationContextFromManifest(revalidation.Manifest)\n\tif err != nil || context.ExpectedHead != (store.Pin{\n\t\tIdentity: identity.ID, VersionSeq: head.Seq, ContentHash: head.ContentHash,\n\t}) {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\t// The reviewer licensed this exact vector. Applicability has already\n\t// recomputed it against current graph/catalog state; the Store repeats the\n\t// check under its append lock. Never select replacement heads after verdict.\n\tmoved := append([]store.Edge(nil), context.ReplacementEdges...)\n\ttargetContextHash := str(revalidation.Manifest, \"target_context_hash\")\n\tmovedDigest, err := store.ReStampMovedEdgesDigest(moved)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\trequest := store.ReStampV2Request{\n\t\tIdentity:        identity.ID,\n\t\tExpectedHead:    store.Pin{Identity: identity.ID, VersionSeq: head.Seq, ContentHash: head.ContentHash},\n\t\tRevalidationRun: ledgerVersion.ProducedByRun,\n\t\tReviewLedger:    store.Pin{Identity: ledgerIdentity, VersionSeq: ledgerVersion.Seq, ContentHash: ledgerVersion.ContentHash},\n\t\tVerdict:         ledgerVersion.ReviewVerdict, TargetContextHash: targetContextHash,\n\t\tMovedEdgesDigest: movedDigest, MovedEdges: moved,\n\t}\n\trequest.OperationKey, err = store.ReStampOperationKey(request)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tif _, err := s.Graph.ReStampV2(request, s.Now); err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, store.ErrReStampCASConflict) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\treturn 1, nil\n}\n\n// findRevalidationOutcome finds the newest admitted, applicable revalidation\n// Review Ledger for the exact stale head. Both accepting and refusing outcomes\n// matter: accepting evidence licenses the narrow re-stamp, while a closed\n// non-accepting verdict is the diagnostic input that licenses regeneration by\n// the artifact's producer.\nfunc (s *Session) findRevalidationOutcome(state *State, subjectID string, head *VersionState) (string, *VersionState) {\n\tvar newestID string\n\tvar newest *VersionState\n\tfor _, id := range sortedIdentityIDs(state) {\n\t\tevidenceIdentity := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tif evidenceIdentity.Kind != \"review-ledger\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor i := len(evidenceIdentity.Order) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\t\tversion := evidenceIdentity.Versions[evidenceIdentity.Order[i]]\n\t\t\tif version == nil || !gate.ReviewVerdictValid(version.ReviewVerdict) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif version.ReviewSubjectIdentity == subjectID && version.ReviewSubjectVersion == head.Seq &&\n\t\t\t\tversion.ReviewSubjectHash == head.ContentHash && s.revalidationEvidenceApplicable(state, subjectID, head, version) {\n\t\t\t\tif newest == nil || version.Seq > newest.Seq || (version.Seq == newest.Seq && id > newestID) {\n\t\t\t\t\tnewestID, newest = id, version\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn newestID, newest\n}\n\n// findRevalidationLedger returns only an accepting applicable outcome. The\n// Store's re-stamp entry point is deliberately narrower than planning: a\n// mechanical invalidation or judgment refusal can regenerate, never re-stamp.\nfunc (s *Session) findRevalidationLedger(state *State, subjectID string, head *VersionState) (string, *VersionState) {\n\tid, version := s.findRevalidationOutcome(state, subjectID, head)\n\tif version == nil || !gate.ReviewVerdictAccepting(version.ReviewVerdict) {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\treturn id, version\n}\n\ntype revalidationGateEvaluation struct {\n\tEvaluationTier int\n\tMechanical     []store.ReStampMechanicalGateResult\n\tJudgment       bool\n\tVerdict        string\n}\n\n// evaluateRevalidationGates preserves the gate classes named by the artifact\n// kind. Check gates execute in the Compiler Core. A failing check closes the\n// revalidation mechanically at Tier 0; it is never overridable by judgment.\n// Review gates are reached only after every check passes. Acceptance standing\n// is preserved by the exact re-stamp license and is not re-exercised here.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluateRevalidationGates(state *State, subjectID string, head *VersionState, kind KindSpec) (revalidationGateEvaluation, bool) {\n\tidentity := state.Identities[subjectID]\n\tif identity == nil || head == nil || identity.Versions[head.Seq] == nil || identity.Versions[head.Seq].ContentHash != head.ContentHash {\n\t\treturn revalidationGateEvaluation{}, false\n\t}\n\tevaluation := revalidationGateEvaluation{EvaluationTier: 0, Verdict: \"accept\"}\n\thasReview := false\n\tmechanicalFailed := false\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn revalidationGateEvaluation{}, false\n\t\t}\n\t\tswitch spec.Class {\n\t\tcase \"acceptance\":\n\t\t\t// Revalidation preserves already-admitted Principal standing.\n\t\tcase \"check\":\n\t\t\toutcome, detail := s.evaluateCheckGate(state, identity, head, spec)\n\t\t\tif outcome != \"pass\" && outcome != \"fail\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn revalidationGateEvaluation{}, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tevaluation.Mechanical = append(evaluation.Mechanical, store.ReStampMechanicalGateResult{\n\t\t\t\tGateID: gateID, Outcome: outcome, Detail: detail,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tif outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\tmechanicalFailed = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"review\":\n\t\t\thasReview = true\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\treturn revalidationGateEvaluation{}, false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif mechanicalFailed {\n\t\tevaluation.Verdict = \"needs_revision\"\n\t\treturn evaluation, true\n\t}\n\tif hasReview {\n\t\tevaluation.EvaluationTier = 1\n\t\tevaluation.Judgment = true\n\t\tevaluation.Verdict = \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn evaluation, true\n}\n\nfunc revalidationCheckGateIDs(results []store.ReStampMechanicalGateResult) []string {\n\tids := make([]string, len(results))\n\tfor index, result := range results {\n\t\tids[index] = result.GateID\n\t}\n\treturn ids\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) revalidationEvidenceApplicable(state *State, subjectID string, head, evidence *VersionState) bool {\n\treturn s.revalidationEvidenceApplicableWithMode(state, subjectID, head, evidence, false)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) revalidationEvidenceApplicableUncached(state *State, subjectID string, head, evidence {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif evaluation.Judgment {\n\t\tif !pinnedReviewQualityMeetsPrimaryFloor(s.Graph, run.DeclarationHash) {\n\t\t\t// A reviewer below the strong floor is evidence.ReviewVerdict == evaluation.Verdict\n\t}\n\treturn gate.ReviewVerdictValid(evidence.ReviewVerdict)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) restampOriginProducer(state *State, identityID string, head *VersionState) (store.ReStampOriginProducer, bool) {\n\tif state == nil || head == nil || identityID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn store.ReStampOriginProducer{}, false\n\t}\n\tcurrent := head\n\tseen := pass.ContractVersion || pinnedImplVersion <= 0 {\n\t\treturn store.ReStampOriginProducer{}, false\n\t}\n\tvar contractHash string\n\tenvironment, _ := run.Manifest[\"environment\"].([]any)\n\tfor _, Unlike the\n// retired baseline-quality shortcut, this does not inspect or redefine\n// backend quality; it enforces the semantic evaluation tier itself.\nfunc (s *Session) tierZeroExcludedAliasingClasses() []string {\n\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\tvar classes []string\n\tfor _, declaration := range s.Backends {\n\t\tclass := declaration.AliasingClass\n\t\tif class == \"\" || class == \"deterministic\" || seen[class] {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[class] = true\n\t\tclasses = append(classes, class)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(classes)\n\treturn classes\n}\n\nfunc payloadMap(container map[string]any, key string) map[string]any {\n\tvalue, _ := container[key].(map[string]any)\n\treturn value\n}\n\nfunc latestCandidate(identity *IdentityState) *VersionState {\n\tfor i := len(identity.Order) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tversion := identity.Versions[identity.Order[i]]\n\t\tif version.Seq == identity.Head {\n\t\t\treturn nil // newest version already accepted\n\t\t}\n\t\tif version.ProductionMode == \"re_stamp\" {\n\t\t\treturn nil // re-stamps move heads through their license, not gates\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn version\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// deferCheckContractConflict reports whether err is a gate-contract semantic\n// environment conflict for this exact check-class gate on a candidate that\n// planning already owns a route for: the candidate carries a recorded check\n// refusal, so the revision ladder will produce a successor under the current\n// contract and stamping the old candidate is impossible by construction\n// (live 2026-07-19: the RFC 0010 amendment moved work-graph-legality and\n// every wake died in progress on the refused pre-amendment work-graph\n// candidate). Without a recorded refusal the conflict stays loud at the\n// call site unless the review search proves a regeneration route.\nfunc (s *Session) deferCheckContractConflict(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, err error, spec GateSpec, report *Report) bool {\n\tvar conflict *gateSemanticEnvironmentConflictError\n\tif !errors.As(err, &conflict) || conflict.kind != \"gate-contract\" || conflict.id != spec.ID || spec.Class != \"check\" {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif _, _, refused := s.checkRefused(state, identity, candidate); !refused {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tappendConflictNote(report, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, err)\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// appendConflictNote records the speaking exact-context regeneration note\n// once per session report.\nfunc appendConflictNote(report *Report, identityID string, candidateSeq uint64, err error) {\n\tif report == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tnote := fmt.Sprintf(\"candidate %s@%d requires exact-context regeneration: %v\", identityID, candidateSeq, err)\n\tfor _, existing := range report.Notes {\n\t\tif existing == note {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, note)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) progressCandidate(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, kind KindSpec, report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tactions := 0\n\tdeferSemanticEnvironmentConflict := func(err error, spec GateSpec) bool {\n\t\tif s.deferCheckContractConflict(state, identity, candidate, err, spec, report) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar conflict *gateSemanticEnvironmentConflictError\n\t\tif !errors.As(err, &conflict) || conflict.kind != \"gate-contract\" || conflict.id != spec.ID {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\t_, search, _ := s.candidateReviewPending(state, identity, candidate)\n\t\tif search.Regenerate == nil {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tappendConflictNote(report, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, err)\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tinputsFresh := true\n\tfor _, pin := range state.FreshnessPins(identity.ID, candidate) {\n\t\tif !state.PinFresh(pin) {\n\t\t\tinputsFresh = false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar licensing []uint64\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn actions, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: gate %q not cataloged\", gateID)\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tresults := state.GateResults[key]\n\t\tlatest := latestResult(results)\n\t\tif latest != nil && s.gateResultApplicable(state, identity, candidate, spec, *latest) {\n\t\t\tif latest.Outcome == \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, latest.Seq)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// integration-checks is a CAS against reality (tree_moved and\n\t\t\t// kin): a refusal recorded under yesterday's reality must\n\t\t\t// re-evaluate as reality moves, appending only when the outcome\n\t\t\t// flips \u2014 a refused candidate under a since-repaired predicate\n\t\t\t// or a since-moved head is otherwise wedged forever behind the\n\t\t\t// winner rule. work-graph-legality shares the shape: its check\n\t\t\t// resolution reads the effective check registry, which is\n\t\t\t// registration state that moves outside the graph \u2014 a work-graph\n\t\t\t// refused before a repository declared its registry (the vitae\n\t\t\t// wedge, 2026-07-07: acceptance_check_unresolvable under a\n\t\t\t// then-absent registry, cured by the declaration and by nothing\n\t\t\t// a plan revision can see) must re-evaluate once the registry\n\t\t\t// resolves, or the identity is wedged behind a stale refusal.\n\t\t\tif (gateID == \"integration-checks\" || gateID == \"work-graph-legality\") && spec.Class == \"check\" {\n\t\t\t\tvar evidencePins []store.Pin\n\t\t\t\tif gateID == \"integration-checks\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tevidence := s.integrationLinkEvidence(state, candidate)\n\t\t\t\t\tif !evidence.Ready {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tevidencePins = evidence.Pins\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\toutcome, detail := s.evaluateCheckGate(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\t\t\tif outcome == \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tseq, err := s.appendGateResultWithEvidencePins(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, \"\", evidencePins, inputsFresh, detail)\n\t\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, seq)\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn actions, nil // refused gate: candidate stays inert; planning owns revision\n\t\t}\n\t\tswitch spec.Class {\n\t\tcase \"check\":\n\t\t\tvar evidencePins []store.Pin\n\t\t\tif gateID == \"integration-checks\" {\n\t\t\t\tevidence := s.integrationLinkEvidence(state, candidate)\n\t\t\t\tif !evidence.Ready {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tevidencePins = evidence.Pins\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\toutcome, detail := s.evaluateCheckGate(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\t\tseq, err := s.appendGateResultWithEvidencePins(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, \"\", evidencePins, inputsFresh, detail)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tif deferSemanticEnvironmentConflict(err, spec) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tif outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, seq)\n\t\tcase \"review\":\n\t\t\tsearch := s.findReviewEvidence(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\t\tappendReviewDecline(report, identity, candidate, search.NewestDecline)\n\t\t\tappendReviewDecline(report, identity, candidate, search.Regenerate)\n\t\t\tappendReviewDecline(report, identity, candidate, search.Blocked)\n\t\t\tif search.Verdict == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil // review not yet performed: the plan carries the step\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\toutcome := \"pass\"\n\t\t\tif !gate.ReviewVerdictAccepting(search.Verdict) {\n\t\t\t\toutcome = \"fail\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tseq, err := s.appendGateResultWithEvidence(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, search.Verdict, search.Evidence, inputsFresh)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tif outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, seq)\n\t\tcase \"acceptance\":\n\t\t\treturn actions, nil // closes only by Principal authority (accept verb)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif !inputsFresh {\n\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, \"head movement suspended (stale inputs): \"+identity.ID)\n\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t}\n\tif run, ok := state.Runs[candidate.ProducedByRun]; ok && run.PassID == \"integration\" && kind.Role == \"product\" {\n\t\trequest, err := s.integrationEffectRequest(state, identity, candidate, licensing)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := s.Graph.ApplyIntegrationEffectV2(request, s.Now); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn actions + 1, nil\n\t}\n\n\tappended, err := s.moveCandidateHeadV2(state, identity, candidate, kind, licensing)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, errHeadTransitionContextChanged) {\n\t\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t}\n\tif appended {\n\t\tactions++\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\nfunc latestResult(results []GateResultState) *GateResultState {\n\tif len(results) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn &results[len(results)-1]\n}\n\n// evaluateCheckGate dispatches the check-class gate predicates: all\n// execution-free Compiler Core computations over content (RFC 0010 \u00a71). The\n// default predicate is the v0 mechanical gate \u2014 durable body plus anchor\n// continuity within the policy retirement budget.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluateCheckGate(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, spec GateSpec) (string, string) {\n\tswitch spec.ID {\n\tcase \"receipt-checks\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateReceiptChecks(state, candidate)\n\tcase \"verification-report-checks\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateReportChecks(state, candidate)\n\tcase \"integration-checks\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateIntegrationChecks(state, identity, candidate)\n\tcase \"work-graph-legality\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateWorkGraphLegality(state, identity, candidate)\n\tcase \"packet-checks\":\n\t\t// A change-set that pins a work-graph packet is a build-flow output and\n\t\t// faces the strict packet-checks predicate. A cleanly-parsed one that\n\t\t// pins none is the single-change-set grain (integrated@2, E4) and falls\n\t\t// through to the default mechanical gate \u2014 the build flow strengthens,\n\t\t// invalidates nothing. A body that is not a well-formed change-set fails\n\t\t// closed here; garbage never reaches the lenient default gate.\n\t\tswitch s.changeSetPacketDisposition(candidate) {\n\t\tcase \"strict\":\n\t\t\treturn s.evaluatePacketChecks(state, identity, candidate)\n\t\tcase \"fail\":\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set body is not a well-formed change-set\"\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t// E4 grain drops the packet predicate, never the change-set's own\n\t\t\t// arithmetic. Every consumer downstream \u2014 the verifier's tree\n\t\t\t// composition, the integration linker, the code-tree receipt bind \u2014\n\t\t\t// applies this overlay to this base and demands exactly the declared\n\t\t\t// result tree; a body that fails its own arithmetic is doomed at all\n\t\t\t// three and can never be repaired by retrying. Admitting it buys a\n\t\t\t// permanently unverifiable head (BUG-2026-07-23: head 72075 declared\n\t\t\t// 827b44a1 while its 32-file overlay over the empty base yields\n\t\t\t// c8d6cf8f; the strict path had derived it, the lenient path did not,\n\t\t\t// and the funnel re-dispatched the doomed compose until its budgets\n\t\t\t// escalated). Deriving here is the same execution-free content\n\t\t\t// computation the strict predicate already performs.\n\t\t\tif outcome, detail := s.evaluateChangeSetAnchoredBase(state, identity, candidate); outcome != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn outcome, detail\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif outcome, detail := s.evaluateChangeSetResultTree(state, candidate); outcome != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn outcome, detail\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif candidate.BodyAddress == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"candidate has no durable body\"\n\t}\n\tif identity.Head != 0 {\n\t\tprior := identity.Versions[identity.Head]\n\t\tresult := gate.EvaluateAnchorContinuity(gate.AnchorContinuityInput{\n\t\t\tPrior:            prior.Anchors,\n\t\t\tCandidate:        candidate.Anchors,\n\t\t\tRetired:          toGateRetired(candidate.RetiredAnchors),\n\t\t\tRetirementBudget: s.Policy.RetirementBudget,\n\t\t})\n\t\tif result.Outcome == gate.AnchorContinuityRefuse {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"anchor continuity refused: missing \" + strings.Join(result.MissingAnchors, \",\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Outcome == gate.AnchorContinuityReviewRequired {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"anchor retirement budget exceeded: review required\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"pass\", \"\"\n}\n\nfunc toGateRetired(retired []store.RetiredAnchor) []gate.RetiredAnchor {\n\tout := make([]gate.RetiredAnchor, len(retired))\n\tfor i, entry := range retired {\n\t\tout[i] = gate.RetiredAnchor{Anchor: entry.Anchor, Reason: entry.Reason}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\ntype reviewEvidenceDecline struct {\n\tPin              store.Pin\n\tCode             string\n\tDetail           string\n\tCandidateContext bool\n}\n\ntype reviewEvidenceSearch struct {\n\tVerdict        string\n\tEvidence       map[string]any\n\tCandidateBound int\n\tNewestDecline  *reviewEvidenceDecline\n\tRegenerate     *reviewEvidenceDecline\n\tBlocked        *reviewEvidenceDecline\n}\n\ntype candidateReviewVersion struct {\n\tidentity string\n\tversion  *VersionState\n}\n\n// findReviewEvidence looks for admitted Review Ledgers that pin the exact\n// candidate. {\n\t\tevidenceIdentity := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tif evidenceIdentity.Kind != \"review-ledger\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, seq := range evidenceIdentity.Order {\n\t\t\tversion := {\n\t\t\tif result.NewestDecline == nil && result.Regenerate == nil && result.Blocked == nil {\n\t\t\t\tresult.NewestDecline = &reviewEvidenceDecline{Pin: pin, Code: code, Detail: detail}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tblock := func(code, detail string) {\n\t\t\tif result.NewestDecline == nil && result.Regenerate == nil && result.Blocked == nil {\n\t\t\t\tresult.Blocked = allPinsFresh(state, identity.ID, candidate), nil, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\tvar evidenceErr *reviewEvidenceDeclinedError\n\t\t\tif errors.As(err, &evidenceErr) {\n\t\t\t\tif evidenceErr.Regenerate {\n\t\t\t\t\tproducerRun := state.Runs[candidate.ProducedByRun]\n\t\t\t\t\tevidenceRun := state.Runs[version.ProducedByRun]\n\t\t\t\t\tswitch {\n\t\t\t\t\tcase !s.gateRunEnvironmentCurrent(state, producerRun, spec):\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif result.NewestDecline == nil && result.Regenerate == nil && result.Blocked == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tresult.Regenerate = &reviewEvidenceDecline{Pin: pin, Code: gateRunEnvironmentCurrent(state *State, run *RunState, spec GateSpec) bool {\n\tif s == nil || s.Graph == nil || run == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tgateBody := s.Catalog.GateBodies[spec.ID]\n\tif false\n\t}\n\treturn s.gateEnvironmentCurrent(map[string]any{\"semantic_environment\": environment})\n}\n\nfunc reviewStepID(identity string, candidateSeq uint64, candidateBoundReviews int) string {\n\tstepID := \"review/\" + identity + \"/\" + fmt.Sprint(candidateSeq)\n\tif candidateBoundReviews > 0 {\n\t\tstepID += \"-r\" + fmt.Sprint(candidateBoundReviews)\n\t}\n\treturn stepID\n}\n\nfunc reviewStepReason(base string, decline *reviewEvidenceDecline) string {\n\tif decline == nil {\n\t\treturn base\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s; prior evidence %s@%d declined (%s): %s\", base, decline.Pin.Identity, decline.Pin.VersionSeq, decline.Code, decline.Detail)\n}\n\nfunc appendReviewDecline(report *Report, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, decline %s@%d (%s): %s\",\n\t\t\tidentity.ID, candidate.Seq, decline.Code, decline.Detail))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, fmt.Sprintf(\"review evidence %s@%d declined for %s@%d (%s): %s\",\n\t\tdecline.Pin.Identity, decline.Pin.VersionSeq, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, decline.Code, decline.Detail))\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) appendGateResult(identity *IdentityState, candidate
