step.Signal),\n\t\t\tPlacement: \"stored\", KindSchemaVersion: 1, Required: true, DerivedFromOutputs: receiptOutputs,\n\t\t})\n\t\treturn out\n\t}\n\tproduces := pass.Produces\n\tif step.PassID == \"review\" {\n\t\tproduces = []string{\"review-ledger\"}\n\t}\n\tfor _, kind := range produces {\n\t\tplacement := \"stored\"\n\t\tidentity := step.Subject\n\t\tif step.PassID == \"review\" {\n\t\t\tidentity = \"evidence/review/\" + sanitize(step.Subject) + \"/\" + step.StepID[strings.LastIndex(step.StepID, \"/\")+1:]\n\t\t}\n\t\tif spec, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[kind]; ok && spec.Role == \"product\" {\n\t\t\tplacement = \"integrated\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tout = append(out, backend.ExpectedOut{\n\t\t\tOutputID: kind, Kind: kind, Identity: identity, Placement: placement,\n\t\t\tKindSchemaVersion: 1, Required: true, DerivedFromOutputs: []string{},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc expectedOutputsValue(expected []backend.ExpectedOut) []any {\n\treturn expectedOutputsValueForSchema(expected, 2)\n}\n\nfunc expectedOutputsValueForSchema(expected []backend.ExpectedOut, schemaVersion int) []any {\n\tout := make([]any, 0, len(expected))\n\tfor _, output := range expected {\n\t\tvalue := map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"output_id\": output.OutputID,\n\t\t\t\"kind\":      output.Kind,\n\t\t\t\"identity\":  output.Identity,\n\t\t\t\"placement\": output.Placement,\n\t\t\t\"required\":  output.Required,\n\t\t}\n\t\tif schemaVersion >= 2 {\n\t\t\tvalue[\"kind_schema_version\"] = int64(output.KindSchemaVersion)\n\t\t\tvalue[\"derived_from_outputs\"] = toAnyStrings(output.DerivedFromOutputs)\n\t\t}\n\t\tout = append(out, value)\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc dispatchInputsValue(inputs []backend.DispatchInput) []any {\n\tout := make([]any, 0, len(inputs))\n\tfor _, input := range inputs {\n\t\tout = append(out, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"identity\": input.Identity, \"version_seq\": int64(input.VersionSeq),\n\t\t\t\"content_hash\": input.ContentHash, \"path\": input.Path,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc stringMapValue(values map[string]string) map[string]any {\n\tout := make(map[string]any, len(values))\n\tfor key, value := range values {\n\t\tout[key] = value\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// laneOccupancy derives per-backend lanes in flight strictly from the\n// ledger fold (D0013.C6): a lane binding whose run has no closure is in\n// flight. Never process liveness; never an advisory read.\nfunc laneOccupancy(state *State) map[string]int {\n\tlanes := map[string]int{}\n\tfor _, run := range state.Runs {\n\t\tif run.Open() && run.BackendID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tlanes[run.BackendID]++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn lanes\n}\n\n// courtesyAbort issues the advisory abort for a superseded dispatch \u2014\n// courtesy ordering under the winner rule, never correctness. A failure is\n// noted and not retried into a guarantee; a degraded abort (deleted hint)\n// is indistinguishable from success here and treated as such.\nfunc (s *Session) courtesyAbort(run *RunState, report *Report) {\n\tif run.DispatchID == \"\" || run.BackendID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tadapter, ok := s.Adapters[run.BackendID]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := adapter.Abort(run.DispatchID); err != nil {\n\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes,\n\t\t\t\"courtesy abort failed for \"+run.DispatchID+\": \"+err.Error())\n\t}\n}\n\n// --- sweep ---\n\nfunc (s *Session) sweep(report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tactions := 0\n\tnow := s.Now().UTC()\n\t// Deterministic backends execute their dispatch synchronously inside the\n\t// drive process (the inline adapter), so a healthy drive commits the\n\t// submission before it exits; an LLM backend hands off to a detached\n\t// supervisor that outlives the drive. That difference is what makes the\n\t// inline-orphan reap below sound for the former and unsound for the\n\t// latter, so it is gated on this set.\n\tinlineBackends := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor _, decl := range s.Backends {\n\t\tif decl.AliasingClass == \"deterministic\" {\n\t\t\tinlineBackends[decl.BackendID] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfor _, runSeq := range sortedRunSeqs(state) {\n\t\trun := state.Runs[runSeq]\n\t\tif !run.Open() || run.OpenedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// A run whose binding escalation the Principal resolved with\n\t\t// `reissue` closes now: nothing was ever launched for it, and the\n\t\t// resolution licenses a fresh attempt.\n\t\treissued := false\n\t\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\t\tif escalation.RunRef == run.Seq && escalation.Resolved && escalation.Disposition == \"reissue\" {\n\t\t\t\treissued = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif reissued {\n\t\t\ts.courtesyAbort(run, report)\n\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"pass_run_closed\", \"driver\", []uint64{run.Seq}, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"run_ref\": int64(run.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\"outcome\": \"superseded_by_redispatch\",\n\t\t\t\t\"detail\":  \"binding escalation resolved: reissue\",\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Legacy v1 cannot reconstruct an absent dispatch bundle because its Run\n\t\t// Manifest did not pin complete materialization. V2 prefixes were rebuilt\n\t\t// at session entry and therefore use only the ordinary horizon path.\n\t\tbundleGone := run.SchemaVersion < 2 && run.DispatchID != \"\" &&\n\t\t\t!pathExists(s.Spool.DispatchDir(run.DispatchID))\n\t\t// Inline-orphan reap: a deterministic-backend run still open with no\n\t\t// committed submission, past a short grace, is an orphan of an\n\t\t// interrupted drive. The inline adapter that would produce its\n\t\t// submission runs synchronously in the drive process; once that drive\n\t\t// is gone no future drive re-executes an already-open run's inline\n\t\t// dispatch (dispatch only fires for un-opened objectives), so the run\n\t\t// can never submit. Reaped without waiting the dispatch horizon, which\n\t\t// exists to let a live detached lane finish \u2014 an inline pass has none.\n\t\t// A committed-but-undrained submission is left alone: the drain path\n\t\t// closes it (finished work is never discarded).\n\t\tif inlineBackends[run.BackendID] && run.DispatchID != \"\" && !bundleGone &&\n\t\t\tnow.Sub(run.OpenedAt) > s.Policy.InlineOrphanGrace &&\n\t\t\t!pathExists(s.Spool.SubmissionDir(run.DispatchID)) {\n\t\t\tif s.abandonedInThis >= s.Policy.AbandonmentCap {\n\t\t\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, \"abandonment cap hit; remaining overdue runs deferred\")\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// A provisional adjudication, never a fact of death (RFC 0006).\n\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"pass_run_closed\", \"driver\", []uint64{run.Seq}, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"run_ref\":         int64(run.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\"outcome\":         \"abandoned\",\n\t\t\t\t\"detail\":          \"inline dispatch orphaned: no submission committed by the drive that opened it\",\n\t\t\t\t\"observed_active\": int64(observedActive(run, now) / time.Second),\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ts.courtesyAbort(run, report)\n\t\t\ts.abandonedInThis++\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !bundleGone && now.Sub(run.OpenedAt) <= s.Policy.DeadlineFor(run.DeadlineClass) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.abandonedInThis >= s.Policy.AbandonmentCap {\n\t\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, \"abandonment cap hit; remaining overdue runs deferred\")\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tif bundleGone {\n\t\t\t// A provisional adjudication, never a fact of death (RFC 0006).\n\t\t\tif _, err := s.appendPassRunClosedV2([]uint64{run.Seq}, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"run_ref\":         int64(run.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\"outcome\":         \"abandoned\",\n\t\t\t\t\"closure_source\":  \"bundle_gone\",\n\t\t\t\t\"closure_reason\":  \"legacy dispatch bundle is gone and its exact materialization cannot be reconstructed\",\n\t\t\t\t\"observed_active\": int64(observedActive(run, now) / time.Second),\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Courtesy abort after the closure is recorded: stop the lapsed\n\t\t\t// lane burning budget; a committed submission stays untouched and\n\t\t\t// still drains provisionally (finished work is never discarded).\n\t\t\ts.courtesyAbort(run, report)\n\t\t\ts.abandonedInThis++\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tdecision := spoolclose.ScanClose(spoolclose.Lane{\n\t\t\tLaneID:         run.LaneID,\n\t\t\tAgeSeconds:     durationSecondsInt(observedActive(run, now)),\n\t\t\tHorizonSeconds: durationSecondsInt(s.Policy.DeadlineFor(run.DeadlineClass)),\n\t\t\tIsWinner:       state.LaneWon(run),\n\t\t})\n\t\tif !decision.Close || decision.Mode != spoolclose.ModeHorizon {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// A provisional adjudication, never a fact of death (RFC 0006).\n\t\tif _, err := s.appendPassRunClosedV2([]uint64{run.Seq}, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"run_ref\":         int64(run.Seq),\n\t\t\t\"outcome\":         \"abandoned\",\n\t\t\t\"closure_source\":  \"horizon\",\n\t\t\t\"scan_close_mode\": string(spoolclose.ModeHorizon),\n\t\t\t\"closure_reason\":  decision.Reason,\n\t\t\t\"observed_active\": int64(observedActive(run, now) / time.Second),\n\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Courtesy abort after the closure is recorded: stop the lapsed\n\t\t// lane burning budget; a committed submission stays untouched and\n\t\t// still drains provisionally (finished work is never discarded).\n\t\ts.courtesyAbort(run, report)\n\t\ts.abandonedInThis++\n\t\tactions++\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\n// --- quiescence ---\n\nfunc (s *Session) quiescenceReason(state *State) string {\n\topenRequests := false\n\tfor _, request := range state.Requests {\n\t\tif request.PlanningActive() && !request.Satisfied && !request.Canceled {\n\t\t\topenRequests = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !openRequests {\n\t\tif len(state.Requests) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn \"satisfied\"\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"idle\"\n\t}\n\tif len(s.acceptanceQueue(state)) > 0 {\n\t\treturn \"awaiting_principal\"\n\t}\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif !escalation.Resolved {\n\t\t\treturn \"awaiting_principal\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfor _, run := range state.Runs {\n\t\tif run.Open() {\n\t\t\treturn \"awaiting_submissions\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"awaiting_capacity\"\n}\n\n// acceptanceQueue lists candidates whose earlier gates closed and whose\n// acceptance gate awaits Principal authority. Only candidates an open\n// compilation request still demands park here: a candidate produced under a\n// since-canceled request stays in the graph \u2014 provisional abandonment never\n// discards finished work \u2014 but it holds no claim on the Principal's\n// attention and no longer pins quiescence at awaiting_principal; a later\n// request over the same subject re-demands it, and admission adopts the\n// version it already won.\nfunc (s *Session) acceptanceQueue(state *State) []QueueItem {\n\tvar queue []QueueItem\n\tfor _, id := range sortedIdentityIDs(state) {\n\t\tif !openRequestDemands(state, id) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tidentity := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tcandidate := latestCandidate(identity)\n\t\tif candidate == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[candidate.Kind]\n\t\tif !ok || kind.Role == \"evidence\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tpendingAcceptance := \"\"\n\t\tready := true\n\t\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\t\tspec := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: id, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\t\tlatest := latestResult(state.GateResults[key])\n\t\t\tapplicable := latest != nil && s.gateResultApplicable(state, identity, candidate, spec, *latest)\n\t\t\tswitch spec.Class {\n\t\t\tcase \"acceptance\":\n\t\t\t\tif !applicable {\n\t\t\t\t\tpendingAcceptance = gateID\n\t\t\t\t} else if latest.Outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tready = false\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\tif !applicable || latest.Outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tready = false\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif ready && pendingAcceptance != \"\" {\n\t\t\tqueue = append(queue, QueueItem{\n\t\t\t\tIdentity: id,\n\t\t\t\tVersion:  candidate.Seq,\n\t\t\t\tKind:     candidate.Kind,\n\t\t\t\tGateID:   pendingAcceptance,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn queue\n}\n\n// openRequestDemands reports whether any open compilation request still\n// demands the identity: the identity is the request's subject or sits under\n// it (every ladder identity is subject-prefixed, N4 \u2014 <subject>/<kind>).\nfunc openRequestDemands(state *State, id string) bool {\n\tfor _, request := range state.Requests {\n\t\tif request.Satisfied || request.Canceled || !request.PlanningActive() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif id == request.Subject || strings.HasPrefix(id, request.Subject+\"/\") {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) nextHorizon(state *State) string {\n\tvar horizon time.Time\n\tfor _, run := range state.Runs {\n\t\tif !run.Open() || run.OpenedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tdeadline := run.OpenedAt.Add(s.Policy.DeadlineFor(run.DeadlineClass))\n\t\tif horizon.IsZero() || deadline.Before(horizon) {\n\t\t\thorizon = deadline\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif horizon.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn horizon.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)\n}\n\nfunc earlierHorizon(left, right string) string {\n\tif left == \"\" {\n\t\treturn right\n\t}\n\tif right == \"\" {\n\t\treturn left\n\t}\n\tleftTime, leftErr := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, left)\n\trightTime, rightErr := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, right)\n\tif leftErr != nil {\n\t\treturn right\n\t}\n\tif rightErr != nil || leftTime.Before(rightTime) {\n\t\treturn left\n\t}\n\treturn right\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) appendSessionRecord(state *State, cursorStart uint64, report Report) (uint64, error) {\n\tdrained := append([]string(nil), report.Drained...)\n\tsort.Strings(drained)\n\tcounts := map[string]any{}\n\tfor phase, count := range report.ActionCounts {\n\t\tcounts[phase] = int64(count)\n\t}\n\tresult, err := s.append(\"drive_session_record\", \"driver\", nil, map[string]any{\n\t\t\"trigger\":           s.Trigger,\n\t\t\"cursor_range\":      []any{int64(cursorStart), int64(state.LastSeq)},\n\t\t\"drained\":           toAnyStrings(drained),\n\t\t\"action_counts\":     counts,\n\t\t\"deferrals\":         []any{},\n\t\t\"next_horizon\":      report.NextHorizon,\n\t\t\"quiescence_reason\": report.Quiescence,\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\treturn result.Seq, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) writeProjections(state *State, report Report) error {\n\tstatus := BuildStatusWithPolicy(state, s.Catalog, s.acceptanceQueue(state), report, s.Policy, s.Now().UTC())\n\tderivedDir := filepath.Join(s.Graph.GraphDir, \"derived\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(derivedDir, 0o700); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\traw, err := json.MarshalIndent(status, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\traw = append(raw, '\\n')\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(derivedDir, \"status.json\"), raw, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\thorizon := map[string]any{\"next_horizon\": report.NextHorizon}\n\thorizonRaw, err := json.MarshalIndent(horizon, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\thorizonRaw = append(horizonRaw, '\\n')\n\treturn os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(derivedDir, \"next-horizon.json\"), horizonRaw, 0o600)\n}\n\n// --- helpers ---\n\nfunc subjectIdentity(subject, kind string) string {\n\treturn subject + \"/\" + kind\n}\n\nfunc sanitize(identity string) string {\n\treturn strings.NewReplacer(\"/\", \"-\", \" \", \"-\").Replace(identity)\n}\n\nfunc workspaceEffect(effects string) string {\n\tif effects == \"E0\" {\n\t\treturn \"E0\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"E1\"\n}\n\nfunc emptySnapshotDigest() string {\n\treturn scheduler.EmptySnapshot().Digest\n}\n\nfunc sha256Hex(raw []byte) string {\n\tsum := sha256.Sum256(raw)\n\treturn hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])\n}\n\nfunc defaultString(value, fallback string) string {\n\tif value == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fallback\n\t}\n\treturn value\n}\n\nfunc toAnySeqs(values []uint64) []any {\n\tout := make([]any, len(values))\n\tfor i, value := range values {\n\t\tout[i] = int64(value)\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc toAnyStrings(values []string) []any {\n\tout := make([]any, len(values))\n\tfor i, value := range values {\n\t\tout[i] = value\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc toAnyList(values []any) []any {\n\tif values == nil {\n\t\treturn []any{}\n\t}\n\treturn values\n}\n\nfunc sortedIdentityIDs(state *State) []string {\n\tids := make([]string, 0, len(state.Identities))\n\tfor id := range state.Identities {\n\t\tids = append(ids, id)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(ids)\n\treturn ids\n}\n\nfunc sortedRequestSeqs(state *State) []uint64 {\n\tseqs := make([]uint64, 0, len(state.Requests))\n\tfor seq := range state.Requests {\n\t\tseqs = append(seqs, seq)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(seqs, func(i, j int) bool { return seqs[i] < seqs[j] })\n\treturn seqs\n}\n\nfunc sortedRunSeqs(state *State) []uint64 {\n\tseqs := make([]uint64, 0, len(state.Runs))\n\tfor seq := range state.Runs {\n\t\tseqs = append(seqs, seq)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(seqs, func(i, j int) bool { return seqs[i] < seqs[j] })\n\treturn seqs\n}\n\n// stageBase writes a product/composed base into a dispatch bundle. An\n// anchored base is expanded at dispatch \u2014 the planning side owns world\n// reads (lanes stay world-blind) \u2014 and travels as two hash-verified\n// inputs: the small anchored pin body at inputs/00-base-pin (the hash\n// change-sets pin) and the expanded tree at inputs/01-base (what lanes\n// materialize). Inline bases pass through unchanged.\nfunc (s *Session) stageBase(inputBodies map[string][]byte, dispatchInputs *[]backend.DispatchInput, baseIdentity string, baseSeq uint64, baseBody []byte, baseHash string) error {\n\tproduct, err := changeset.ParseProduct(baseBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif product.Anchor == nil {\n\t\tinputBodies[\"inputs/01-base\"] = baseBody\n\t\t*dispatchInputs = append(*dispatchInputs, backend.DispatchInput{\n\t\t\tIdentity:    baseIdentity,\n\t\t\tVersionSeq:  baseSeq,\n\t\t\tContentHash: baseHash,\n\t\t\tPath:        \"inputs/01-base\",\n\t\t})\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\texpanded, err := s.expandAnchoredProduct(product)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tstageBasePin(inputBodies, dispatchInputs, baseIdentity, baseSeq, baseBody, baseHash)\n\treturn stageExpandedBase(inputBodies, dispatchInputs, baseIdentity, expanded)\n}\n\n// stageBatchBase writes the observed product head at the batch-integration\n// seam. Inline products retain the existing batch-base input. Anchored products\n// use the same pin-plus-expanded-tree pair as single-change integration, but an\n// unexpandable anchor deliberately travels as pin-only: the local linker owns\n// the typed anchored-base guard, so the Driver must preserve that condition\n// rather than substitute the overlay or turn it into unrelated exhaust.\nfunc (s *Session) stageBatchBase(inputBodies map[string][]byte, dispatchInputs *[]backend.DispatchInput, baseIdentity string, baseSeq uint64, baseBody []byte, baseHash string) error {\n\tproduct, err := changeset.ParseProduct(baseBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif product.Anchor == nil {\n\t\tinputBodies[\"inputs/batch-base\"] = baseBody\n\t\t*dispatchInputs = append(*dispatchInputs, backend.DispatchInput{\n\t\t\tIdentity:    baseIdentity,\n\t\t\tVersionSeq:  baseSeq,\n\t\t\tContentHash: baseHash,\n\t\t\tPath:        \"inputs/batch-base\",\n\t\t})\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tstageBasePin(inputBodies, dispatchInputs, baseIdentity, baseSeq, baseBody, baseHash)\n\texpanded, err := s.expandAnchoredProduct(product)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn stageExpandedBase(inputBodies, dispatchInputs, baseIdentity, expanded)\n}\n\nfunc stageBasePin(inputBodies map[string][]byte, dispatchInputs *[]backend.DispatchInput, baseIdentity string, baseSeq uint64, baseBody []byte, baseHash string) {\n\tinputBodies[\"inputs/00-base-pin\"] = baseBody\n\t*dispatchInputs = append(*dispatchInputs, backend.DispatchInput{\n\t\tIdentity:    baseIdentity + \"/pin\",\n\t\tVersionSeq:  baseSeq,\n\t\tContentHash: baseHash,\n\t\tPath:        \"inputs/00-base-pin\",\n\t})\n}\n\nfunc stageExpandedBase(inputBodies map[string][]byte, dispatchInputs *[]backend.DispatchInput, baseIdentity string, expanded changeset.Product) error {\n\texpandedBody, err := changeset.CanonicalProduct(expanded)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\texpandedHash, err := changeset.TreeHash(expanded)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tinputBodies[\"inputs/01-base\"] = expandedBody\n\t*dispatchInputs = append(*dispatchInputs, backend.DispatchInput{\n\t\tIdentity:    baseIdentity,\n\t\tContentHash: expandedHash,\n\t\tPath:        \"inputs/01-base\",\n\t})\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc pathExists(path string) bool {\n\t_, err := os.Stat(path)\n\treturn err == nil\n}\n\n// harvestArtifact resolves the artifact identity a harvest subject names:\n// \"<artifact-identity>/harvest/<posture>\" \u2014 the convention that lets the\n// delegation policy bound agent issuance by subject pattern while the\n// conjuncts still reach the real artifact.\nfunc harvestArtifact(subject string) string {\n\tif idx := strings.Index(subject, \"/harvest/\"); idx > 0 {\n\t\treturn subject[:idx]\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// reviewAdmittedUnder finds a Review Ledger over the given head produced by\n// a run of this request \u2014 the non-vacuous core of the harvest review\n// posture: old reviews never satisfy a new request.\nfunc (s *Session) reviewAdmittedUnder(state *State, request *RequestState, head *VersionState) *VersionState {\n\tfor _, id := range sortedIdentityIDs(state) {\n\t\tevidence := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tif evidence.Kind != \"review-ledger\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor i := len(evidence.Order) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\t\tversion := evidence.Versions[evidence.Order[i]]\n\t\t\tif version.ReviewSubjectHash != head.ContentHash {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif run, ok := state.Runs[version.ProducedByRun]; ok && run.RequestRef == request.Seq {\n\t\t\t\treturn version\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// CurrentState exposes the current fold for read-side consumers (harvest\n// issuance cadence and candidate selection) \u2014 derived, never authoritative.\nfunc (s *Session) CurrentState() (*State, error) {\n\treturn s.fold()\n}\n\n// versionsSinceLicense counts an identity's versions created after the\n// latest resolved bounds escalation naming it \u2014 the revision-budget twin of\n// the redispatch licensing rule.\nfunc versionsSinceLicense(state *State, identity *IdentityState, subject string) int {\n\tvar licensedAfter uint64\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif escalation.Resolved && escalation.CandidatePin.Identity == \"\" && strings.Contains(escalation.Detail, subject) &&\n\t\t\tescalation.Seq > licensedAfter {\n\t\t\tlicensedAfter = escalation.Seq\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor _, seq := range identity.Order {\n\t\tif seq > licensedAfter {\n\t\t\tcount++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn count\n}\n\nfunc candidateBoundReviewRevisionsSinceLicense(state *State, subject string, versionSeq uint64, contentHash string) int {\n\tvar licensedAfter uint64\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif escalation.Resolved && escalation.Reason == \"bounds_exhausted\" && escalation.BudgetKind == \"revision\" &&\n\t\t\tescalation.BlockingScope == subject && escalation.CandidatePin.Identity == subject &&\n\t\t\tescalation.CandidatePin.VersionSeq == versionSeq && escalation.CandidatePin.ContentHash == contentHash &&\n\t\t\tescalation.Disposition == \"proceed\" {\n\t\t\tcursor := escalation.ResolutionRef\n\t\t\tif cursor == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tcursor = escalation.Seq\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif cursor > licensedAfter {\n\t\t\t\tlicensedAfter = cursor\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor _, identity := range state.Identities {\n\t\tif identity.Kind != \"review-ledger\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, seq := range identity.Order {\n\t\t\tversion := identity.Versions[seq]\n\t\t\tif version == nil || version.Seq <= licensedAfter || version.ReviewSubjectIdentity != subject ||\n\t\t\t\tversion.ReviewSubjectVersion != versionSeq || version.ReviewSubjectHash != contentHash {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcount++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn count\n}\n\n// --- schedule ---\n\n// schedule is the post-quiescence planner-scheduling phase\n// (planner-auto-schedules-the-backlog@1): walk the declared backlog in\n// declared order (C5 \u2014 slice index only, no map iteration, no randomness,\n// no clock) and append at most Policy.BacklogCapPerDrive planner-provenance\n// compilation requests toward named target states that are neither\n// satisfied nor under active request (C3, C4, C7). Deterministic\n// bookkeeping over declared state, by construction: the phase appends ONLY\n// compilation_request records \u2014 it contains no call that appends a\n// gate_result, resolution_event, acceptance, or any other authority record,\n// and no code path that writes the backlog file (C1, C12).\nfunc (s *Session) schedule() (int, error) {\n\tif len(s.Backlog) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\t// Satisfied and active sets key on the target-state identifier alone\n\t// (C3/C4), derived from the existing request fold: satisfied when any\n\t// folded request toward the target carries Satisfied, actively requested\n\t// when any folded request toward it is neither Satisfied nor Canceled.\n\t// The active set is a mutable working set for the remainder of this\n\t// pass: each issuance inserts its target before the next entry is\n\t// inspected, so a later backlog entry naming the same target \u2014 under the\n\t// same or a different subject \u2014 skips under C4 within this same drive,\n\t// and one drive never appends two planner requests toward one\n\t// target-state identifier regardless of cap.\n\tsatisfied := map[string]bool{}\n\tactive := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor _, request := range state.Requests {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase request.Satisfied:\n\t\t\tsatisfied[request.TargetID] = true\n\t\tcase request.PlanningActive() && !request.Canceled:\n\t\t\tactive[request.TargetID] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tissued := 0\n\tfor index, entry := range s.Backlog {\n\t\tif s.backlogIssuedInThis >= s.Policy.BacklogCapPerDrive {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tif satisfied[entry.Target] || active[entry.Target] {\n\t\t\tcontinue // skipped entries consume no cap (C6)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// One selected entry, one captured request (C8), through the shared\n\t\t// issuance path \u2014 an uncataloged or conjunct-less backlog target\n\t\t// refuses here exactly as it would for a principal.\n\t\tif _, err := IssueRequest(s.Graph, s.Catalog, RequestOptions{\n\t\t\tSubject:            entry.Subject,\n\t\t\tTargetID:           entry.Target,\n\t\t\tNote:               fmt.Sprintf(\"planner-scheduled from the declared backlog (entry %d)\", index),\n\t\t\tNow:                s.Now,\n\t\t\tInstance:           s.Instance,\n\t\t\tIssuerKind:         \"planner\",\n\t\t\tScheduledFromEntry: index,\n\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn issued, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tactive[entry.Target] = true\n\t\ts.backlogIssuedInThis++\n\t\tissued++\n\t}\n\treturn issued, nil\n}\n",
    "internal/driver/subject_assembly.go": "package driver\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/changeset\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/workgraph\"\n)\n\n// subjectAssemblyDisposition classifies a subject-grain change-set demand\n// against the subject's accepted Work Graph (RFC 0010 work-graph-integrated@2,\n// clause subject-assembly-is-deterministic). It is the live lowering\n// condition the cataloged subject-assembly pass declaration left for a later\n// pass to wire (catalog/passes/subject-assembly.yaml's closing note):\n//\n//   - subjectAssemblyOutOfRange: the demand does not fit subject-assembly's\n//     declared codomain at all \u2014 no fresh accepted Work Graph head, a\n//     legacy-era (v1) body with no element-grain packet pins, or a\n//     single-packet graph the ordinary per-packet producer already builds\n//     directly without ever risking a from-scratch whole-delivery rebuild.\n//     The generic producer resolves exactly as it always has.\n//   - subjectAssemblyDeferred: a multi-packet Work Graph whose packet\n//     frontier is still admitting heads. The lawful path forward is the\n//     built:work-graph conjunct's own per-packet frontier (planWorkGraphBuild),\n//     never a duplicate whole-subject production objective.\n//   - subjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable: a packet the composition needs is\n//     quarantined (D0010.C9, attempt budget exhausted) or the graph's\n//     authored application order does not resolve \u2014 the composition cannot\n//     complete under the current graph state without escalation resolving\n//     the blocker first.\n//   - subjectAssemblyApplicable: every packet head the graph names is an\n//     admitted, fresh, non-rejected accepted head. subject-assembly composes\n//     them deterministically over the anchored base; the basis carries the\n//     complete five-part D0010.C5 preimage.\ntype subjectAssemblyDisposition int\n\nconst (\n\tsubjectAssemblyOutOfRange subjectAssemblyDisposition = iota\n\tsubjectAssemblyDeferred\n\tsubjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable\n\tsubjectAssemblyApplicable\n)\n\nfunc (d subjectAssemblyDisposition) String() string {\n\tswitch d {\n\tcase subjectAssemblyDeferred:\n\t\treturn \"deferred\"\n\tcase subjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable:\n\t\treturn \"unsatisfiable\"\n\tcase subjectAssemblyApplicable:\n\t\treturn \"applicable\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"out_of_range\"\n\t}\n}\n\n// subjectAssemblyBasis is the complete five-part D0010.C5 base-composition\n// preimage a subject-assembly Change Set repeats verbatim: the immutable\n// Work Graph lineage (identity, version hash), the exact observed Product\n// occurrence, every admitted packet head in Work Graph index order, and the\n// resulting composed-base hash their fold yields. changeset.AssemblyPacketPin\n// and changeset.AssemblyBaseComposition render it into the v2 wire shape.\ntype subjectAssemblyBasis struct {\n\tWorkGraphIdentity    string\n\tWorkGraphVersionHash string\n\tObservedProduct      changeset.ArtifactPin\n\tHeads                []changeset.IndexedChangeSetPin\n\tResultingBaseHash    string\n}\n\n// PacketPin renders the basis's immutable lineage as the v2 subject-grain\n// packet pin (changeset.GrainSubjectAssembly): the same Work Graph identity\n// and version hash a packet-grain pin carries, with no single packet anchor\n// or element hash, since the composed set is exactly Heads.\nfunc (b subjectAssemblyBasis) PacketPin() changeset.PacketPin {\n\treturn changeset.AssemblyPacketPin(b.WorkGraphIdentity, b.WorkGraphVersionHash)\n}\n\n// BaseComposition renders the basis's base-composition preimage, validated\n// through the identical validateBaseComposition arithmetic every packet-grain\n// change-set goes through (changeset.AssemblyBaseComposition).\nfunc (b subjectAssemblyBasis) BaseComposition() (*changeset.BaseComposition, error) {\n\treturn changeset.AssemblyBaseComposition(b.ObservedProduct, b.Heads, b.ResultingBaseHash)\n}\n\n// classifySubjectAssembly resolves every packet head of the subject's\n// accepted Work Graph exactly once \u2014 a single pass over the graph's authored\n// application index \u2014 and classifies the subject-grain change-set demand it\n// stands over. It is consulted from the driver's ordinary change-set demand\n// seam (computeSteps's generic producer branch) so that seam never falls to\n// a from-scratch producer lane while a multi-packet Work Graph's admitted\n// packet heads already carry the composition \u2014 the RQ-85343 counterexample\n// work-graph-integrated@2 retires: nine production attempts where the\n// generic ensure walk dispatched one lane to rebuild an entire 22-packet\n// delivery from scratch inside a horizon shorter than its own dispatch\n// budget, while composePacketBaseFold and the batch linker sat unused beside\n// it.\nfunc (s *Session) classifySubjectAssembly(state *State, request *RequestState) (subjectAssemblyDisposition, *subjectAssemblyBasis, string) {\n\tsubject := request.Subject\n\twgIdentity := subjectIdentity(subject, \"work-graph\")\n\twgHead := state.HeadVersion(wgIdentity)\n\tif wgHead == nil || wgHead.Stale {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyOutOfRange, nil, \"no fresh accepted work-graph head\"\n\t}\n\tbody, err := s.getObject(wgHead.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyOutOfRange, nil, err.Error()\n\t}\n\twg, err := workgraph.Parse(body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyOutOfRange, nil, err.Error()\n\t}\n\tif wg.SchemaVersion != 2 {\n\t\t// A pre-v2 Work Graph carries no element-grain packet pins for\n\t\t// AssemblyPacketPin/AssemblyBaseComposition to bind; its packetless\n\t\t// single-change-set path (planLegacyChangeSetWorkGraph) already\n\t\t// handles it \u2014 out of subject-assembly's declared codomain, never a\n\t\t// wait.\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyOutOfRange, nil, \"legacy-era work-graph carries no v2 element-grain packet pins\"\n\t}\n\tif len(wg.Packets) <= 1 {\n\t\t// A single-packet delivery has nothing to compose: the ordinary\n\t\t// per-packet producer already builds it directly and the from-\n\t\t// scratch-whole-delivery failure mode does not arise (assembly-\n\t\t// serialized-closure's named deferral covers the batch-union\n\t\t// arithmetic across concurrent subjects this does not attempt).\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyOutOfRange, nil, \"single-packet work-graph: ordinary production applies\"\n\t}\n\tindex, err := workgraph.ApplicationIndex(wg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// A Work Graph whose authored order does not resolve can never\n\t\t// fold \u2014 a structural dead end for composition, not a wait.\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable, nil, fmt.Sprintf(\"work-graph application index does not resolve: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tpositions := make(map[string]int64, len(index))\n\tfor position, anchor := range index {\n\t\tpositions[anchor] = int64(position)\n\t}\n\tproductID := productIdentity(subject)\n\texcluded := s.quarantinedPackets(state, request, wg)\n\n\tvar heads []changeset.IndexedChangeSetPin\n\tvar quarantined []string\n\tvar pending []string\n\tfor _, packetID := range index {\n\t\tcsID := packetChangeSetIdentity(subject, packetID)\n\t\thead := state.HeadVersion(csID)\n\t\tif head == nil || head.Stale {\n\t\t\tif excluded[packetID] {\n\t\t\t\tquarantined = append(quarantined, packetID)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tpending = append(pending, packetID)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.applicationBound(state, request, csID, head, productID) {\n\t\t\t// Already folded into the observed Product by a prior\n\t\t\t// integration; not a pending ancestor, and re-listing it in\n\t\t\t// Heads would double-declare content the base composition\n\t\t\t// already carries (mirrors packetBaseComposition).\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, rejected := s.verificationRejected(state, csID, head); rejected {\n\t\t\t// A conclusively-rejected head is not admitted: its packet\n\t\t\t// frontier is already routing a revision (mirrors the built[]\n\t\t\t// disposition planWorkGraphBuild computes).\n\t\t\tpending = append(pending, packetID)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\theads = append(heads, changeset.IndexedChangeSetPin{\n\t\t\tApplicationIndex: positions[packetID],\n\t\t\tChangeSet: changeset.ArtifactPin{\n\t\t\t\tIdentity: csID, VersionSeq: head.Seq, ContentHash: head.ContentHash,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tif len(quarantined) > 0 {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable, nil, fmt.Sprintf(\n\t\t\t\"packet(s) quarantined (attempt budget exhausted, D0010.C9): %s\", strings.Join(quarantined, \", \"))\n\t}\n\tif len(pending) > 0 {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyDeferred, nil, fmt.Sprintf(\n\t\t\t\"%d of %d packet heads not yet admitted\", len(pending), len(wg.Packets))\n\t}\n\n\tobserved, err := s.observedProductPin(state, subject)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable, nil, err.Error()\n\t}\n\t_, resultingHash, err := s.composeSubjectAssemblyBase(state, subject, heads)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn subjectAssemblyUnsatisfiable, nil, err.Error()\n\t}\n\treturn subjectAssemblyApplicable, &subjectAssemblyBasis{\n\t\tWorkGraphIdentity:    wgIdentity,\n\t\tWorkGraphVersionHash: wgHead.ContentHash,\n\t\tObservedProduct:      observed,\n\t\tHeads:                heads,\n\t\tResultingBaseHash:    resultingHash,\n\t}, \"\"\n}\n\n// composeSubjectAssemblyBase folds every admitted packet head (heads, already\n// in Work Graph index order) onto the observed Product tree: the flat batch\n// fold (changeset.ApplyBatch) rather than composePacketBaseFold's per-\n// ancestor recursion. Each admitted head already passed packet-checks\n// against its own composed base (D0010.C5), so this is exactly the union\n// D0010.C8's batch linker performs at integration, computed here purely to\n// classify and pin the demand \u2014 never to move a head or stage a lane\n// (subject-assembly's effects: E0).\nfunc (s *Session) composeSubjectAssemblyBase(state *State, subject string, heads []changeset.IndexedChangeSetPin) (changeset.Product, string, error) {\n\tbase, err := s.observedProductBase(state, subject)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, \"\", err\n\t}\n\tchanges := make([]changeset.ChangeSet, 0, len(heads))\n\tfor _, head := range heads {\n\t\tversion := state.HeadVersion(head.ChangeSet.Identity)\n\t\tif version == nil {\n\t\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"driver: subject-assembly head %s has no admitted version\", head.ChangeSet.Identity)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcsBody, err := s.getObject(version.ContentHash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, \"\", err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcs, err := s.parseChangeSet(csBody)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, \"\", err\n\t\t}\n\t\tchanges = append(changes, cs)\n\t}\n\tresult, conflicts := changeset.ApplyBatch(base, changes)\n\tif len(conflicts) > 0 {\n\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"driver: subject-assembly base fold: %s\", conflicts[0].Detail)\n\t}\n\thash, err := changeset.TreeHash(result)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn changeset.Product{}, \"\", err\n\t}\n\treturn result, hash, nil\n}\n\n// subjectAssemblyInputs renders a basis's ordered admitted-head identities as\n// the planStep.Inputs a subject-assembly produce step pins: the Work Graph\n// head followed by every composed packet change-set identity, in application\n// order. subject-assembly's own cataloged `consumes: change-set` names the\n// composed packet-grain set it folds, never this demand's own subject-grain\n// identity \u2014 the generic producer.Consumes walk (subjectIdentity(subject,\n// consumed)) would otherwise self-reference the very identity being\n// produced, so the live lowering seam builds this list explicitly instead of\n// delegating to that generic walk.\nfunc subjectAssemblyInputs(basis *subjectAssemblyBasis) []string {\n\tinputs := make([]string, 0, len(basis.Heads)+1)\n\tinputs = append(inputs, basis.WorkGraphIdentity)\n\tfor _, head := range basis.Heads {\n\t\tinputs = append(inputs, head.ChangeSet.Identity)\n\t}\n\treturn inputs\n}\n",

want out_of_range\", disposition)\n\t}\n\tif basis != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"out-of-range disposition carried a non-nil basis: %+v\", basis)\n\t}\n}\n\n// No accepted Work Graph head at all is equally *testing.T) {\n\tsession, state := newSubjectAssemblyFixture(t)\n\trequest := &RequestState{Seq: 1, Subject: \"s\"}\n\n\tdisposition, basis, _ := session.classifySubjectAssembly(state, request)\n\tif disposition != subjectAssemblyOutOfRange {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"disposition = %v, want out_of_range\", disposition)\n\t}\n\tif basis != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"out-of-range disposition carried a non-nil its own (RQ-85343: no from-scratch producer lane, ever).\nfunc TestClassifySubjectAssemblyDefersWhileFrontierAdmits(t *testing.T) {\n\tsession, state := \"one\\n\"})\n\t// p2 has not admitted yet.\n\trequest := &RequestState{Seq: 1, Subject: \"s\"}\n\n\tdisposition, basis, detail := session.classifySubjectAssembly(state, request)\n\tif disposition != subjectAssemblyDeferred {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"disposition = %v (%s), want deferred\", disposition, detail)\n\t}\n\tif basis != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"deferred disposition carried a non-nil basis: %+v\", basis)\n\t}\n}\n\n// A packet whose attempt budget is exhausted (D0010.C9 quarantine) makes the\n// whole-subject composition unsatisfiable under current graph state \u2014 a\n// distinct classification from an ordinary in-progress wait.\nfunc TestClassifySubjectAssemblyUnsatisfiableWhenPacketQuarantined(t *testing.T) {\n\tsession, state := newSubjectAssemblyFixture(t)\n\tadmitWorkGraph(t, session, state, \"s\", twoPacketWorkGraph())\n\tadmitPacketChangeSet(t, session, state, \"s\", \"p1\", map[string]string{\"build/p1.txt\": request)\n\tif disposition != subjectAssemblyApplicable {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"disposition = %v (%s), want applicable\", disposition, detail)\n\t}\n\tif basis == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"applicable disposition index 0\", basis.Heads[0])\n\t}\n\tif basis.Heads[1].ChangeSet.Identity != \"s/packets/p2/change-set\" || basis.Heads[1].ApplicationIndex != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"basis.Heads[1] = %+v, want p2 at application index 1\", basis.Heads[1])\n\t}\n\tif basis.ResultingBaseHash == \"\" {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"basis.ResultingBaseHash is empty\")\n\t}\n\n\tcomposition, err := basis.BaseComposition()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"BaseComposition() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif composition.ResultingBaseHash != basis.ResultingBaseHash || len(composition.Ancestors) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"BaseComposition() = %+v\", composition)\n\t}\n\tpin := basis.PacketPin()\n\tif pin.Grain != changeset.GrainSubjectAssembly || pin.PacketID != \"\" || pin.PacketElementHash != \"\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"PacketPin() = %+v, want subject-grain with no single packet anchor\", pin)\n\t}\n\n\t// The resulting base hash is exactly the batch fold of both admitted\n\t// heads over the observed tree (empty: no product lineage %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif basis.ResultingBaseHash != wantHash {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ResultingBaseHash = %s, want %s\", basis.ResultingBaseHash, wantHash)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc fixtureCatalog() Catalog {\n\treturn \"s\"}\n\ttarget := TargetSpec{ID: \"t\", Conjuncts: []Conjunct{{Kind: \"accepted_head\", ArtifactKind: \"change-set\"}}}\n\n\tunmet, steps := session.computeSteps(state, request, target)\n\t// Planning a step never retroactively satisfies the same drive's\n\t// conjunct \u2014 exactly like every other produce/revise branch in\n\t// computeSteps, the demand stays unmet until the staged run admits a\n\t// head on a later drive.\n\tif len(unmet) != 1 || unmet[0] != \"accepted_head:change-set\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmet = %v, want [accepted_head:change-set]\", unmet)\n\t}\n\tif len(steps) != step.PassID)\n\t}\n\tif step.Subject != \"s/change-set\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Subject = %q, want s/change-set\", step.Subject)\n\t}\n\twant := []string{\"s/work-graph\", \"s/packets/p1/change-set\", \"s/packets/p2/change-set\"}\n\tif !reflect.DeepEqual(step.Inputs, want) {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Inputs = %v, want %v\", step.Inputs, want)\n\t}\n}\n\n// The counterexample this delivery retires \"build\" \u2014 that is the lane dispatch that burned three\n// horizon abandonments rebuilding an entire delivery inside a budget shorter\n// than its own dispatch horizon. The demand simply stays unmet; the\n// built:work-graph conjunct's own per-packet frontier is what makes\n// progress.\nfunc TestComputeStepsNeverDispatchesFromScratchBuildWhileFrontierAdmits(t session, state, \"s\", \"p1\", map[string]string{\"build/p1.txt\": \"one\\n\"})\n\t// p2 is still building.\n\trequest := &RequestState{Seq: 1, Subject: \"s\"}\n\ttarget := TargetSpec{ID: \"t\", Conjuncts: []Conjunct{{Kind: \"accepted_head\", ArtifactKind: \"change-set\"}}}\n\n\tunmet, steps := session.computeSteps(state, request, target)\n\tif len(unmet) != 1 || unmet[0] != TestComputeStepsFallsBackToGenericProducerForSinglePacketGraph(t *testing.T) {\n\tsession, state := newSubjectAssemblyFixture(t)\n\tsession.Catalog = fixtureCatalog()\n\tadmitWorkGraph(t, session,
